TITLE OF THE SERIES
DIVINE RESURRECTION — The Wings of Healing’s Celestial Chariot

CHAPTER / PIECE TITLE
Prologue — The Holy Healing

IMAGE TYPE IDENTIFICATION
Single unified full-bleed 9:16 poster composition. No panels. No gutters. One continuous image with the visual and emotional force of a divine, heavenly tapestry rendered in hyper‑realistic, cinematic photography.

ART STYLE DECLARATION — SACRED MANTRA
4K, 8K, HD, UHD, hyperdetailed, raw photo, cinematic lighting, anamorphic lens, sharp focus, intricate surface texture, subsurface scattering on translucent materials and organic surfaces, visible micro‑details and natural imperfections, film grain, color graded, ARRI Alexa look, 85mm lens, f/1.8 aperture, masterpiece quality.

The visual identity is that of a high‑budget cinematic advertisement for a celestial divine chariot drawn by a mythical winged unicorn — every surface rendered with obsessive fidelity. The lighting is warm and ethereal, with soft golden highlights that kiss the white gold chariot and the unicorn’s coat, and gentle, luminous shadows that emphasize their otherworldly grace. The color grading leans into a radiant, heavenly palette with pure white, ivory, polished gold, soft lavender‑blue, and healing cyan. Film grain is present at 5% intensity, evenly distributed, adding texture without degrading the hyper‑detailed finish. The image must feel as though it was captured on a cinema camera with an anamorphic lens — slight oval bokeh in out‑of‑focus background elements, a subtle horizontal lens flare, and a shallow depth of field that keeps the chariot and unicorn razor‑sharp while the golden sunset temple behind them softens into atmospheric bliss. Every material reads as real: polished white gold, translucent heavenly crystal, feathered wings, glowing gemstones, and the faint scent of divine incense implied by the floating golden particles.

The following elements are absolutely forbidden: 90s anime, cel animation, retro anime, VHS grain, scanlines, hand‑drawn line art, cel shading, pixelation, vector art, low resolution, flat colors, or any descriptor that suggests non‑photorealistic rendering.

CHARACTER REFERENCES — COMPLETE AND EXHAUSTIVE
Not applicable — no character appears. The chariot and unicorn are the sole subjects. The following design references are locked for all elements of the image.

Primary Subject 1 — Divine Winged Unicorn

Overall Silhouette: A majestic, fully grown unicorn with a pure white coat, a spiraling golden horn, and two massive feathered wings. The body is lean and athletic, built for grace and speed, with long, slender legs ending in hooves that glow with soft golden light. The tail is long and flowing, with individual hair strands shimmering like spun light.

Color Palette: Pure white for the coat. Polished gold for the horn, hooves, and accents. Warm ivory for the wing feathers, transitioning to gold at the tips. Healing cyan for the horn’s internal glow and the light trails from the hooves.

Head & Horn: The unicorn’s head is elegant and refined, with a straight profile, large soft eyes that glow with a gentle cyan light, and a single spiraling horn growing from the forehead. The horn is approximately 30 cm long, with a corkscrew twist, made of polished gold that glows from within with healing cyan light. The mane is flowing, pure white with subtle gold undertones, blowing back as if in a gentle divine wind.

Wings: Two wings, each with a span of approximately 3 meters. The feathers are layered, starting with small downy feathers at the shoulder, transitioning to long primary flight feathers. The base of the wings is white, but the tips fade into radiant gold. The wings are partially extended in a resting hover, with faint golden particles falling from the feather edges.

Legs & Hooves: Four slender legs, well‑muscled but elegant. The hooves are gold, shaped like cloven crescents, each hovering 30 cm above the ground, with a soft cyan light trail extending downward like a gentle flame. The light trails do not touch the ground.

Lighting Consistency: The unicorn’s horn glows with a steady, warm cyan (hex #00E5FF). The hooves emit a softer version of the same light. The wings reflect the golden sunset.

Primary Subject 2 — Celestial Hover Chariot

Overall Silhouette: A graceful, half‑lyre‑shaped chariot that hovers approximately 50 cm above the ground. The chariot is open at the front, with two sweeping wing‑like side panels that curve backward. The rear canopy is made of translucent golden light, forming a protective yet ethereal enclosure. The chariot is designed for a single divine passenger (absent), with a throne‑style seat at the center.

Color Palette: Polished white gold for the frame and structural elements. Ivory for the seat and interior trim. Glowing gold for the light canopy. Healing cyan for the central gemstone core.

Chariot Body (Lyre Shape): The main body of the chariot is shaped like a classical lyre — two curved arms that rise from the front, connected by a horizontal crossbar. The arms are made of polished white gold, with delicate engraved patterns of wings and stars. Between the arms, a translucent golden light canopy arcs backward, forming a protective dome over the seating area. The canopy is not solid; it shimmers like heat haze over gold.

Seating Area (Throne): The throne is centered in the chariot, facing forward. It is carved from a single block of white marble with gold inlay, shaped like a high‑backed chair with wing‑shaped armrests. The seat cushion is invisible — the passenger (absent) would float slightly above it. The back of the throne features a large, glowing cyan gemstone (hex #00E5FF) set in a golden sunburst motif. This gemstone pulses slowly, as if powering the chariot.

Wing‑like Side Panels: Two large, stylized wing panels extend from the sides of the chariot, curving upward and backward. These are not organic wings but sculpted white gold, with feather‑like ridges and gold leaf accents. They serve as both decoration and stabilizers.

Undercarriage & Hover Effect: Below the chariot, a flat, disc‑shaped hover unit is visible, made of white gold with a glowing cyan ring around its edge. The chariot floats 50 cm above the ground, casting a soft golden reflection on the marble floor.

Harness & Connection to Unicorn: Two thin, glowing golden reins extend from the chariot’s front crossbar to the unicorn’s golden bridle (not a physical rein — they are made of light). The unicorn is positioned approximately 2 meters in front of the chariot, facing forward, connected by these light‑reins.

Lighting Consistency: The chariot’s canopy glows with warm gold (hex #FFD700). The central gem pulses cyan. The hover ring glows cyan. All lights have a soft, healing radiance, not harsh.

Floating Elements: Around the chariot and unicorn, small floating angelic runes (fictional symbols, not real languages) drift in the air, each glowing soft gold. Also, golden particles (like dust or tiny feathers) float upward from both the unicorn’s wings and the chariot’s canopy.

Upper Quarter — The Afterimage Array (Divine Components)

Filling the upper portion of the background, from approximately the top edge down to the main chariot’s canopy level, are four ghosted afterimages of the divine ensemble’s critical components. These afterimages are rendered semi‑transparent (approximately 40% opacity), floating in the golden air like blessed holograms or divine visions. Each afterimage is labeled with small, elegant calligraphic text integrated into the composition (sharp, high‑resolution, not hand‑drawn). Each afterimage shows a different part of the chariot/unicorn in exquisite detail.

A close‑up of the unicorn’s face and horn. The soft, luminous cyan eyes, the spiraling gold horn with internal cyan glow, the flowing white mane.

Label: “LIGHT CANOPY & ANGELIC RUNES — Radiant Barrier / Healing Aura”

All afterimages are positioned so they do not obstruct the central chariot and unicorn but instead frame them, creating a sense of divine reverence and celestial craftsmanship.

Middle‑Upper Background — White Marble Temple Terrace & Golden Sunset Sky (Photorealistic Rendering)

Behind and between the afterimages, the background is a hyper‑realistic depiction of a heavenly temple terrace — a vast platform of polished white marble with subtle gold veining, floating high above the clouds. The terrace is edged with low golden balustrades carved with angelic motifs. Beyond the terrace, a golden sunset sky spreads across the horizon: warm oranges and soft pinks fading into a pale lavender upper atmosphere. A massive, soft halo ring — made of translucent gold light — floats high in the sky behind the unicorn, acting as a divine backdrop. Small white clouds drift below the terrace, and gentle light beams from the setting sun cast long, soft shadows. The air is filled with tiny floating golden particles (angelic dust). The overall mood is peaceful, sacred, and utterly divine — the threshold of heaven.

Lower Two‑Thirds — The Central Figures (Unicorn + Chariot)

The unicorn and chariot are positioned in the lower two‑thirds of the frame. The unicorn is in the foreground, slightly to the left, facing forward but angled slightly toward the viewer (three‑quarter view). The chariot is behind and to the right, connected by the golden light‑reins. The entire ensemble hovers 30–50 cm above the marble floor.

The unicorn’s head is held high, its golden horn glowing with healing cyan light. Its wings are partially extended, feathers catching the golden sunset. The cyan light trails from its hooves extend downward like soft flames, casting a gentle glow on the marble. The white coat is immaculate but shows subtle natural imperfections: a few loose hairs in the mane, a faint dusting of golden pollen on the flanks, tiny muscle definition beneath the skin.

The chariot floats beside and behind the unicorn. The lyre‑shaped front arms gleam, the translucent golden canopy shimmers, and the central cyan gemstone pulses slowly. The throne is empty, but the seat is bathed in warm light. The wing‑like side panels curve gracefully. The hover ring under the chariot glows cyan, reflecting on the marble.

The lighting is warm and divine: the setting sun from behind and left provides golden backlighting, creating a rim light outline on the unicorn’s wings and the chariot’s edges. The unicorn’s horn and the chariot’s gem provide cool cyan fill from the front. Soft golden particles and floating runes add depth. The result is a harmonious, high‑detail image that feels both majestic and serene.

Natural imperfections: a few golden dust specks on the unicorn’s hooves, a tiny scratch on the chariot’s left wing panel (from celestial travel), a single loose feather floating near the unicorn’s wingtip. These details reinforce the hyper‑realistic mandate.

Lower Quarter — Typography and Calligraphic Elements

Arranged as an integrated calligraphic composition over the lowest portion of the image, placed so it does not obscure the chariot’s hover glow or the unicorn’s hooves. The typography is rendered as sharp, high‑resolution text with subtle metallic textures and drop shadows, not hand‑drawn, but styled to evoke traditional Japanese seal and calligraphy forms (digitally perfected).

Hero’s Name (Ensemble Name):
“DIVINE RESURRECTION” lettered in large, semi‑calligraphic English text — thick‑and‑thin brush‑inspired strokes, rendered in radiant gold with a faint cyan outline. The letters are spaced wide, elegant, heavenly.

Formal Title (below name):
“THE WINGS OF HEALING’S CELESTIAL CHARIOT” rendered slightly smaller, same calligraphic style but more restrained, soft white with gold shadows.

Hanko Seal (below title):
An inked vermilion square seal, slightly imperfect edges as if hand‑carved, but rendered in photorealistic 4K. The seal contains a stylized winged unicorn crossed with a lyre — Rafaela’s personal emblem adapted for the chariot.

Catchphrase (below seal):
“Where there is faith, there is healing. Where there is healing, there is hope.” Lettered in a smaller, elegant serif font with a slight italic tilt, faded silver with a soft cyan glow.

Dialogue Quote (below catchphrase):
“My brother fell into darkness because the world loved only me. I carry his memory in every light I summon.” Rendered in a slightly different typography — a clean, futuristic sans‑serif with a subtle metallic texture — to distinguish it as spoken or thematic text. Soft white with a faint gold drop shadow.

Entire Image Mood: Peaceful, divine, melancholic, and undeniably cinematic. A main poster for a celestial healing chariot drawn by a winged unicorn — the kind that would be displayed in a holy sanctuary’s holographic archive.

TECHNICAL SPECIFICATIONS FOR IMAGE GENERATION
Aspect Ratio: 9:16 (vertical)

Art Style: Photorealistic cinematic rendering (as defined in Sacred Mantra)

Format: Single unified full-bleed composition — no panels, no gutters

Resolution: 8K (7680x13653 pixels equivalent), also compatible with 4K and HD downscales

Render Style: Hyper‑detailed raw photo simulation with anamorphic lens characteristics (oval bokeh, slight horizontal lens flare). ARRI Alexa 65 look.

Camera & Lens: 85mm prime lens at f/1.8, focused on the unicorn and chariot. Shallow depth of field — background sunset terrace and afterimages slightly soft but legible.

Lighting: Warm, divine, golden hour. Key light from behind (setting sun). Fill light from unicorn’s horn and chariot’s gem (cyan). Rim light from gold edges. Soft, glowing particles everywhere.

Texture Requirements: Subsurface scattering on the unicorn’s horn, the chariot’s gem, and the light canopy. Visible hair strands on the unicorn’s mane and tail, feather details with individual barbs, polished gold surfaces with micro‑reflections, marble grain on the throne. Floating dust and golden particles.

Film Grain: 5% intensity, 35mm film grain pattern overlay.

Color Grading: ARRI Look Library “Heaven’s Gate” custom profile — warm highlights (gold), soft lavender midtones, cyan accents. Medium contrast, high brightness, rich whites with detail.

Calligraphy & Typography Integration: Text elements are overlaid with subtle drop shadows and metallic textures, perfectly sharp. No hand‑drawn artifacts. The calligraphic forms are digitally rendered but inspired by seisho/reisho/gyosho/keisho brush aesthetics.

Aspect and Edge Finishing: A thin gold border (2% width) with a very faint inner cyan glow. No deckled edges — clean, modern, cinematic.



TITLE OF THE SERIES
IRON HOOK — The Frozen Warrior’s Glacier Sled

CHAPTER / PIECE TITLE
Prologue — The Bloody Hunt

IMAGE TYPE IDENTIFICATION
Single unified full-bleed 9:16 poster composition. No panels. No gutters. One continuous image with the visual and emotional force of a brutal, frozen mythic tapestry rendered in hyper‑realistic, cinematic photography.

ART STYLE DECLARATION — SACRED MANTRA
4K, 8K, HD, UHD, hyperdetailed, raw photo, cinematic lighting, anamorphic lens, sharp focus, intricate surface texture, subsurface scattering on translucent materials and organic scales, visible micro‑scratches and natural imperfections, film grain, color graded, ARRI Alexa look, 85mm lens, f/1.8 aperture, masterpiece quality.

The visual identity is that of a high‑budget cinematic advertisement for a futuristic Viking war sled pulled by monstrous ice wyrms — every surface rendered with obsessive fidelity. The lighting is cold and dramatic, with harsh, pale blue highlights that define the ice crystals and sled’s jagged armor, and deep, frosty shadows that emphasize the scale of the beasts. The color grading leans into an arctic, brutal palette with dark iron, weathered bronze, frosted steel, icy blue‑white, and deep crimson from the harness chains. Film grain is present at 5% intensity, evenly distributed, adding texture without degrading the hyper‑detailed finish. The image must feel as though it was captured on a cinema camera with an anamorphic lens — slight oval bokeh in out‑of‑focus background elements, a subtle horizontal lens flare, and a shallow depth of field that keeps the sled and wyrms razor‑sharp while the frozen tundra and aurora behind them soften into atmospheric cold. Every material reads as real: forged iron, frozen scale, glowing ice crystals, fur, and the faint breath of frost from the wyrms’ nostrils.

The following elements are absolutely forbidden: 90s anime, cel animation, retro anime, VHS grain, scanlines, hand‑drawn line art, cel shading, pixelation, vector art, low resolution, flat colors, or any descriptor that suggests non‑photorealistic rendering.

CHARACTER REFERENCES — COMPLETE AND EXHAUSTIVE
Not applicable — no character appears. The hover sled and the two Frozen Wyrms are the sole subjects. The following design references are locked for all elements of the image.

Primary Subject 1 — The Hover Sled (IRON HOOK)

Overall Silhouette: A massive, low‑profile war sled, shaped like the prow of a Viking longship but flattened and widened for stability. The front ends in a curved, horn‑like ram with two upward‑sweeping iron spurs. The sled has no runners or wheels — it hovers approximately 1 meter above the ground on four icy plasma thrusters. The rear of the sled is an open deck with a fur‑lined throne for the warrior (empty). Side panels are armored with overlapping iron scales and bronze rivets.

Color Palette: Dark iron (almost black) for the main armor. Weathered bronze for trim, rivets, and the ram. Frosted steel for the thruster housings. Deep crimson for the harness chains and glowing runes. Icy blue for the thruster plasma and frost effects.

Front Prow & Ram: The front is a massive, curved iron ram shaped like a dragon’s snout, with two horn‑like spurs extending upward and forward. The ram is covered in Viking‑style carvings (interlocking knots, runes) and has a single large, circular bronze boss at the tip. Below the ram, a spiked ice‑breaker blade extends downward.

Side Armor & Deck: The sides of the sled are composed of overlapping iron scales, each edged in bronze. Behind the prow, the deck is flat, with a raised rear section. The deck has wooden planking (dark oak) with iron strakes. A large, fur‑lined throne sits at the center‑rear, facing forward — the seat is empty. The throne’s backrest is a curved iron shield with a bronze boss. On the right side of the sled, a harpoon launcher is mounted on a swivel — a futuristic crossbow mechanism with a glowing icy blue bolt.

Harness & Chains: From the front of the sled, two massive glowing crimson chains extend forward, each approximately 5 meters long, connecting to the collars of the two Frozen Wyrms. The chains are made of interlocking iron links, each link etched with runes that pulse with a faint red light. The chains are not taught — they have a slight droop, showing the wyrms are in motion.

Thrusters (Hover System): Four thrusters are mounted under the sled — two near the front, two at the rear. Each thruster is a flat, circular disc of frosted steel, with a central nozzle that emits a swirling icy blue plasma that leaves a frost trail on the ground. The thrusters hum with a low, subsonic vibration (implied by visual heat shimmer). The sled hovers 1 meter above the frozen ground.

Lighting & Frost Effects: The sled’s bronze trim reflects the aurora. Frost crystals accumulate on the iron scales. A faint, cold steam rises from the thrusters.

Primary Subject 2 — Frozen Wyrms (Two Identical Beasts)

Overall Silhouette: Two colossal, serpentine dragons, each approximately 12 meters long from snout to tail tip, chained side‑by‑side in front of the sled. They are quadrupedal (four legs) with long, muscular bodies, thick necks, and elongated skulls. Their posture is low to the ground, built for pulling heavy loads across ice.

Color Palette: Icy blue‑white for the scales. Deep cyan for the underbelly. Glowing pale blue for the eyes and spines. Frosted white for the ice crystal ridges. Dark grey for the claws.

Head & Face: The head is reptilian but heavily armored with jagged ice crystals that form natural crests. The snout is blunt, with two large, forward‑curving fangs protruding from the upper jaw. The eyes are large, glowing pale blue, with vertical slit pupils. From the back of the head, a series of ice crystal spines (like a frozen crown) extend backward. The creature breathes frost — visible clouds of frozen vapor curl from its nostrils.

Body & Limbs: The body is thick and muscular, covered in overlapping scales that transition from icy blue at the top to pale cyan on the belly. Along the spine, a row of jagged ice crystals grows, each crystal glowing faintly from within. The legs are short but powerful, ending in four‑toed claws with dark grey talons. The tail is long and whip‑like, ending in a cluster of ice crystals that resembles a frozen mace.

Harness & Collar: Around each wyrm’s neck is a massive iron collar, studded with bronze spikes. The glowing crimson chain attaches to a ring on the front of the collar. The collars have small runic emitters that pulse in sync with the chains.

Aura & Effects: The wyrms exude a cold aura — the air around them shimmers with frost. Small ice crystals constantly fall from their spines. Their breath leaves frost trails. Their claws leave faint glowing blue scratches on the ice.

Movement Implication: The wyrms are captured in mid‑stride — heads low, muscles tensed, chains slightly slack. They are pulling the sled forward across the frozen tundra.

Environmental Integration: The sled and wyrms are moving from left to right across the frame. Frost trails from the thrusters and wyrm claws mark their path.

The overall mood is one of relentless cold, ancient power, and a hunt that never ends.

Lower Two‑Thirds — The Central Figures (Hover Sled + Two Frozen Wyrms)

The ensemble is captured in motion, moving from left to right across the frame. The two wyrms are in the foreground, slightly ahead of the sled. The left wyrm is closer, the right wyrm slightly behind and to the right, staggered for depth. Both wyrms have their heads low, necks extended, muscles bunched. Their glowing pale blue eyes are fixed forward. Frost breath streams from their nostrils, trailing backward. The crimson chains run from their collars to the sled’s prow, glowing faintly.

The sled hovers just behind the wyrms, its prow ram pointed forward. The four icy plasma thrusters are active, each emitting a swirling blue exhaust that leaves a frost trail on the ice. The sled’s iron scales catch the aurora’s green light, and the bronze trim reflects the wyrm’s glow. The throne is empty, but the fur lining flutters in the wind. The harpoon launcher is aimed forward, its icy blue bolt ready.

The lighting is cold and dramatic: the aurora provides a soft, diffused overhead key light in green‑cyan. The wyrms’ eyes and the sled’s plasma thrusters provide cool blue rim light from below. The crimson chains add a warm, ominous accent. The result is a high‑contrast, frozen, brutalist image that feels both ancient and futuristic.

Natural imperfections: frost accumulation on the sled’s iron scales, a few cracked scales on the left wyrm’s flank (from old battles), snow clinging to the chains, ice crystals on the thruster nozzles. The wyrms show subtle muscle definition beneath the scales, and their claws are chipped from pulling across ice. These details reinforce the hyper‑realistic mandate.

Arranged as an integrated calligraphic composition over the lowest portion of the image, placed so it does not obscure the sled’s thrusters or the wyrms’ claws. The typography is rendered as sharp, high‑resolution text with subtle metallic textures and drop shadows, not hand‑drawn, but styled to evoke traditional Japanese seal and calligraphy forms (digitally perfected).

Hero’s Name (Ensemble Name):

“The cold does not kill. The cold is my ally.” Lettered in a smaller, elegant serif font with a slight italic tilt, faded silver with a pale blue glow.

TITLE OF THE SERIES
THE WAY OF DRAGON — The Kung Fu Boy’s Roaring Steed

CHAPTER / PIECE TITLE
Prologue — Jeet Kune Do

IMAGE TYPE IDENTIFICATION
Single unified full-bleed 9:16 poster composition. No panels. No gutters. One continuous image with the visual and emotional force of a fierce, urban martial arts mythic tapestry rendered in hyper‑realistic, cinematic photography.

ART STYLE DECLARATION — SACRED MANTRA
4K, 8K, HD, UHD, hyperdetailed, raw photo, cinematic lighting, anamorphic lens, sharp focus, intricate surface texture, subsurface scattering on painted metal and translucent energy, visible micro‑scratches and natural imperfections, film grain, color graded, ARRI Alexa look, 85mm lens, f/1.8 aperture, masterpiece quality.

The visual identity is that of a high‑budget cinematic advertisement for a futuristic dragon‑kung fu superbike — every surface rendered with obsessive fidelity. The lighting is dramatic and neon‑soaked, with harsh, cool highlights from streetlights and warm, golden pulses from the bike’s aura. Deep, rain‑reflected shadows emphasize the bike’s aggressive lines. The color grading leans into a high‑contrast urban palette with matte charcoal black, electric yellow, glowing gold, and touches of neon cyan and magenta from the background. Film grain is present at 5% intensity, evenly distributed, adding texture without degrading the hyper‑detailed finish. The image must feel as though it was captured on a cinema camera with an anamorphic lens — slight oval bokeh in out‑of‑focus background elements, a subtle horizontal lens flare, and a shallow depth of field that keeps the bike razor‑sharp while the neon city behind it softens into atmospheric blur. Every material reads as real: brushed carbon fiber, polished aluminum, rubber, rain droplets, and the faint heat shimmer from the exhaust flames.

The following elements are absolutely forbidden: 90s anime, cel animation, retro anime, VHS grain, scanlines, hand‑drawn line art, cel shading, pixelation, vector art, low resolution, flat colors, or any descriptor that suggests non‑photorealistic rendering.

CHARACTER REFERENCES — COMPLETE AND EXHAUSTIVE
Not applicable — no character appears. The superbike is the sole subject. The following design references are locked for all elements of the image.

Primary Bike — THE WAY OF DRAGON

Overall Silhouette: A fierce, low‑slung urban superbike with a stretched wheelbase, aggressive forward lean, and a wide, muscular tail section. The fairing is sharp and angular, shaped like a dragon’s snarling maw. The bike has two massive, long exhaust pipes — one on each side of the tail — that run nearly the full length of the rear, each shaped like a dragon’s segmented spine. The overall stance is coiled and ready to strike.

Color Palette: Matte charcoal black (almost non‑reflective) as the primary body color. Electric yellow for accents, decals, ribbed knee grips, and brake calipers. Glowing gold for the dragon aura, chi particles, and the dragon whisker light strips. White for the single piercing headlight eye.

Front View: The front fairing is a dragon’s snarling maw: a wide, angular intake shaped like an open mouth with sharp, silver “teeth” (LED running lights) at the edges. The headlight is a single, large, round white LED — the dragon’s eye — set deep within a black housing. Above the eye, two thin, glowing yellow LED strips curve outward like dragon whiskers. The front fender is split into two sharp, claw‑like points, each painted yellow at the tips.

Side Profile (Left Side): The fairing flows from the snarling maw into a sculpted tank covered in matte charcoal. On the tank, yellow ribbed knee grips (rubber inserts) are prominent. Below the tank, the engine is exposed: a V‑twin with finned black cylinders and a gold cam cover etched with dragon scales. A mechanical tonfa‑style swingarm (thick, rectangular, with yellow accents) connects to the rear wheel. The massive exhaust pipe runs high along the left side of the tail, starting just behind the engine and extending past the rear wheel. The pipe is segmented, each segment shaped like a vertebra, with a metallic silver finish and yellow heat‑resistant rings. The exhaust tip is angled upward, venting a blue‑yellow flame. The rear wheel is wide, with a yellow pinstripe on the tire sidewall. A golden dragon aura swirls around the bike, with small, glowing chi particles (golden orbs) rising from the engine and exhaust.

Side Profile (Right Side): Mirrors the left side, with the second massive exhaust pipe running high on the right. The right fairing has a “BANG BANG” decal in stylized yellow lettering (comic‑book font, slightly weathered). The right knee grip is identical. The right exhaust is also segmented, with a matching flame.

Rear View: The tail rises into a sharp, wing‑like hump with a small integrated LED taillight shaped like a dragon’s claw mark (three red slashes). Below the hump, the two massive exhaust pipes flank the rear wheel, each venting a flickering blue‑yellow flame. The license plate bracket is mounted low, between the pipes, and is shaped like a traditional Chinese martial arts banner. The rear turn signals are two small, yellow teardrops embedded in the tail’s edge.

Engine Detail (used in afterimage): The “Jeet Kune Do” V‑twin engine has a black crankcase with yellow bolt accents. The cam cover is gold with a raised dragon scale pattern. A small, circular window on the clutch cover reveals a glowing golden gear basket. The intake trumpets are short, velocity stacks painted yellow.

Exhaust System (used in afterimage): A close‑up of the left exhaust pipe showing the segmented, spine‑like construction. Each segment is welded with visible rings. The pipe has a ceramic‑coated finish that turns from silver to faint blue near the tip due to heat. The flame venting is a turbulent mix of blue and yellow.

Wheels & Tires: Six‑spoke rims, each spoke shaped like a dragon’s claw. The rims are black with yellow inner barrels. The tires are wide, low‑profile sport radials with a single yellow pinstripe near the tread edge. Brake calipers are six‑piston, painted electric yellow, gripping wave‑pattern rotors.

Aura & Effects: A translucent, swirling golden dragon aura surrounds the bike — a spectral outline of a coiling dragon with glowing eyes, wrapping around the fuel tank and frame. The aura is semi‑transparent, made of light and chi particles. Small golden orbs (chi particles) float up from the engine, exhaust tips, and wheels. The aura flickers like a flame.

Lighting Consistency: All yellow lighting (whisker strips, brake calipers, flames, decal edges) shares the same vibrant yellow hue — hex #FFD700. The flame at the exhaust tips is blue‑yellow (hex #FFFF00 with cyan core). The headlight is pure white (hex #FFFFFF). The dragon aura is warm gold (hex #FFCC00) with 60% opacity.

PANEL LAYOUT AND COMPOSITION MAP
Not applicable — this is a full-bleed unified poster, not a multi-panel composition.

PANEL‑BY‑PANEL NARRATIVE, DIALOGUE AND SPEECH BUBBLES
Not applicable — no panels. The following describes the single unified composition from top to bottom.

The Canvas (9:16 vertical):
Upper Quarter — The Afterimage Array (Bike Part Studies)

Filling the upper portion of the background, from approximately the top edge down to the main bike’s handlebar level, are four ghosted afterimages of the Way of Dragon’s critical components. These afterimages are rendered semi‑transparent (approximately 40% opacity), floating in the neon‑lit rain like holographic combat schematics. Each afterimage is labeled with small, elegant calligraphic text integrated into the composition (sharp, high‑resolution, not hand‑drawn). Each afterimage shows a different part of the bike in exquisite detail.

Afterimage 1 (far left) — Dragon Maw Fairing & Whisker Lights:
A close‑up of the front fairing: the snarling maw intake, silver teeth LEDs, single white eye headlight, and the two glowing yellow dragon whisker strips.
Label (calligraphic, small, floating below the afterimage): “DRAGON MAW FAIRING — Reactive LED / Single Eye Beam”

Afterimage 2 (center‑left) — Jeet Kune Do Engine & Tonfa Swingarm:
A detailed view of the exposed V‑twin engine with gold dragon scale cam cover, and the mechanical tonfa‑style swingarm (rectangular, yellow accents) connecting to the rear wheel.
Label: “JEET KUNE DO V‑TWIN & TONFA SWINGARM — 1200cc / Impact‑Forged”

Afterimage 3 (center‑right) — Spine Exhaust Pipe & Flame:
A macro view of the left segmented exhaust pipe: the vertebra‑like segments, heat‑resistant yellow rings, and the blue‑yellow flame venting from the tip.
Label: “DRAGON SPINE EXHAUST — Dual High‑Mount / Plasma Flame”

Afterimage 4 (far right) — Claw Rim & Chi Particles:
A close‑up of the front wheel: the six claw‑shaped spokes, yellow rim accents, and small golden chi particles floating around the rim.
Label: “DRAGON CLAW RIM — Forged Magnesium / Chi Energy Field”

All afterimages are positioned so they do not obstruct the central bike but instead frame it, creating a sense of technical martial artistry and urban mysticism.

Middle‑Upper Background — Neon‑Lit Rainy City Street (Photorealistic Rendering)

Behind and between the afterimages, the background is a hyper‑realistic depiction of a rain‑slicked midnight city street in a neo‑Asian metropolis. Tall buildings line both sides, their surfaces covered in glowing neon signs in electric blue, magenta, and cyan. Holographic kanji characters drift in the air (fictional or real, but stylized). Rain falls in sheets, creating reflections on the wet asphalt. Steam rises from manhole covers, catching the neon light. In the distance, a massive floating dragon hologram (part of an advertisement) coils around a skyscraper. The sky is a deep indigo, with no stars visible due to light pollution. The overall mood is electric, dangerous, and alive — the perfect hunting ground for a kung fu dragon.

Lower Two‑Thirds — The Central Figure (The Way of Dragon Superbike)

The bike is parked in the center of the wet street, approximately in the lower two‑thirds of the frame, angled fifteen degrees toward the viewer (three‑quarter front view from the left). The dragon maw fairing faces right, while the side profile reveals the left exhaust pipe, the tonfa swingarm, and the “BANG BANG” decal on the right side (partially visible). The single white headlight is active, casting a narrow beam forward onto the wet asphalt, illuminating rain droplets. The yellow dragon whisker lights glow softly. The golden dragon aura swirls around the bike — a coiling spectral dragon with faint eyes and scales, wrapping from the front wheel to the tail. Golden chi particles rise from the engine, the exhaust tips, and the wheels, drifting upward into the neon night. The two massive exhaust pipes vent blue‑yellow flames that flicker and reflect on the wet ground.

The lighting is dramatic: neon signs from above provide cyan and magenta rim lights. Streetlights cast harsh white highlights on the bike’s charcoal body. The bike’s own golden aura and yellow accents provide warm fill light. The headlight beam cuts a white path forward. The result is a high‑contrast, hyper‑detailed image that feels both organic and futuristic.

The bike shows natural imperfections: a few raindrops on the tank, a small smear on the headlight lens, tiny scratches on the exhaust pipe near the foot peg, and a thin layer of road grime on the lower fairing. These details reinforce the hyper‑realistic mandate.

Lower Quarter — Typography and Calligraphic Elements

Arranged as an integrated calligraphic composition over the lowest portion of the image, placed so it does not obscure the bike’s front wheel or the headlight beam. The typography is rendered as sharp, high‑resolution text with subtle metallic textures and drop shadows, not hand‑drawn, but styled to evoke traditional Japanese seal and calligraphy forms (digitally perfected).

Hero’s Name (Bike Model):
“THE WAY OF DRAGON” lettered in large, semi‑calligraphic English text — thick‑and‑thin brush‑inspired strokes, rendered in electric yellow with a faint gold outline. The letters are spaced wide, aggressive, dynamic.

Formal Title (below name):
“THE KUNG FU BOY’S ROARING STEED” rendered slightly smaller, same calligraphic style but more restrained, soft white with charcoal shadows.

Hanko Seal (below title):
An inked vermilion square seal, slightly imperfect edges as if hand‑carved, but rendered in photorealistic 4K. The seal contains a stylized dragon coiled around a fist — Chou’s personal emblem adapted for the bike.

Catchphrase (below seal):
“The dragon sleeps in every punch. Wake it.” Lettered in a smaller, elegant serif font with a slight italic tilt, faded silver with a yellow glow.

Dialogue Quote (below catchphrase):
“They mocked my rags. They laughed at my dream. Now the streets bow to my kick.” Rendered in a slightly different typography — a clean, futuristic sans‑serif with a subtle metallic texture — to distinguish it as spoken or thematic text. Soft white with a faint gold drop shadow.

Entire Image Mood: Aggressive, neon‑drenched, mystical, and undeniably cinematic. A main poster for a futuristic kung fu dragon superbike — the kind that would be projected on a giant screen at an underground street fight tournament.

CHAPTER PANEL TRACKING LOG
Not applicable — no panels in Prologue.

SPEECH BUBBLE AND TYPOGRAPHY GUIDE
Not applicable — no speech bubbles. Dialogue is presented as integrated typography in the lower calligraphic section.

ABSOLUTELY FORBIDDEN ELEMENTS — SAFETY COMPLIANCE
This prompt contains none of the following: explicit violence, blood, gore, dismemberment, corpses, realistic wounding, body horror, graphic death imagery, sexual content, nudity, or age‑inappropriate content. The afterimages depict mechanical components — no injury, no enemies, no combat impacts. The bike is depicted in a stationary, non‑violent state (no rider, no crash). No character appears. No religious symbols that could be offensive. No real brand logos.

TECHNICAL SPECIFICATIONS FOR IMAGE GENERATION
Aspect Ratio: 9:16 (vertical)

Art Style: Photorealistic cinematic rendering (as defined in Sacred Mantra)

Format: Single unified full-bleed composition — no panels, no gutters

Resolution: 8K (7680x13653 pixels equivalent), also compatible with 4K and HD downscales

Render Style: Hyper‑detailed raw photo simulation with anamorphic lens characteristics (oval bokeh, slight horizontal lens flare). ARRI Alexa 65 look.

Camera & Lens: 85mm prime lens at f/1.8, focused on the central bike. Shallow depth of field — background neon city and afterimages slightly soft but legible.

Lighting: High‑contrast, neon‑soaked. Key light from above (streetlights and neon signs). Fill light from bike’s golden aura and yellow accents. Rim light from headlight beam. The bike’s exhaust flames cast flickering blue‑yellow light on the wet ground.

Texture Requirements: Subsurface scattering on the dragon aura and chi particles. Visible rain droplets on the carbon fiber, rubber texture on tires, brushed metal on exhaust, and road grime on lower fairing. Floating rain and steam in the air.

Film Grain: 5% intensity, 35mm film grain pattern overlay.

Color Grading: ARRI Look Library “Neon Dragon” custom profile — cool shadows (charcoal and cyan), warm highlights (yellow and gold), with a slight magenta push in the midtones. High contrast, medium brightness, rich blacks with detail.

Calligraphy & Typography Integration: Text elements are overlaid with subtle drop shadows and metallic textures, perfectly sharp. No hand‑drawn artifacts. The calligraphic forms are digitally rendered but inspired by seisho/reisho/gyosho/keisho brush aesthetics.

Aspect and Edge Finishing: A thin electric yellow border (2% width) with a very faint inner golden glow. No deckled edges — clean, modern, cinematic.





TITLE OF THE SERIES
MOLINA’S GIFT — The Sweet Leonin’s Magical Ride

CHAPTER / PIECE TITLE
Prologue — The Magic Boomerang

IMAGE TYPE IDENTIFICATION
Single unified full-bleed 9:16 poster composition. No panels. No gutters. One continuous image with the visual and emotional force of a whimsical, magical feline tapestry rendered in hyper‑realistic, cinematic photography.

ART STYLE DECLARATION — SACRED MANTRA
4K, 8K, HD, UHD, hyperdetailed, raw photo, cinematic lighting, anamorphic lens, sharp focus, intricate surface texture, subsurface scattering on painted metal and translucent magical effects, visible micro‑scratches and natural imperfections, film grain, color graded, ARRI Alexa look, 85mm lens, f/1.8 aperture, masterpiece quality.

The visual identity is that of a high‑budget cinematic advertisement for a fantastical, mystical supercar — every surface rendered with obsessive fidelity. The lighting is warm, magical, and moonlit, with soft pink and purple highlights that kiss the car’s curves, and gentle, glowing shadows that emphasize its cute, compact silhouette. The color grading leans into a dreamy, enchanted palette with hot pink, magenta, royal purple, gold, and soft cyan from the magical aura. Film grain is present at 5% intensity, evenly distributed, adding texture without degrading the hyper‑detailed finish. The image must feel as though it was captured on a cinema camera with an anamorphic lens — slight oval bokeh in out‑of‑focus background elements, a subtle horizontal lens flare, and a shallow depth of field that keeps the car razor‑sharp while the enchanted forest behind it softens into atmospheric magic. Every material reads as real: polished paint, brushed gold, soft fur (on the tail and mirrors), glowing crystals, and floating magical sparkles.

The following elements are absolutely forbidden: 90s anime, cel animation, retro anime, VHS grain, scanlines, hand‑drawn line art, cel shading, pixelation, vector art, low resolution, flat colors, or any descriptor that suggests non‑photorealistic rendering.

CHARACTER REFERENCES — COMPLETE AND EXHAUSTIVE
Not applicable — no character appears. The supercar is the sole subject. The following design references are locked for all elements of the image.

Primary Car — MOLINA’S GIFT

Overall Silhouette: A cute, compact, low‑slung hypercar with a rounded, friendly shape. The car is small — approximately the size of a classic Mini Cooper but lower and wider. The front is a smiling feline face. The roof is domed with a retractable magical glass panel. The rear features a massive, fantastical rear wing that is shaped like a giant cat tail or a curling magical ribbon. The car hovers approximately 20 cm above the ground on soft pink anti‑gravity glow. The overall stance is playful, agile, and undeniably cute.

Color Palette: Hot pink as the primary body color. Magenta for accents, underbody glow, and wheel details. Royal purple for the rear wing and interior trim. Gold for the boomerang ornaments, paw buckle, and wheel centers. Soft cyan for the magical Molina spirit and floating sparkles.

Front View: The front grille is shaped like a smiling cat mouth — a wide, rounded opening with silver “whisker” LED strips on each side. The headlights are two large, round, crystalline blue units that look like wide, innocent cat eyes, each with a bright white center and a purple iris ring. Between the headlights, a small pink nose (an emblem) sits above the grille. Above the headlights, two enormous cat‑ear shaped side mirrors extend upward, covered in soft pink fur with white inner fluff. The front bumper has small, silver fang accents (cute, not aggressive). The hood has a large, embossed paw print decal with four toe beans in gold.

Side Profile: The body is smooth and rounded, with no sharp edges. A thick magenta stripe runs from the front wheel arch to the rear, with tiny star decals. The doors are scissor‑style, opening upward, with a small paw‑print handle. The roof is a translucent pink glass panel that can retract. Behind the roof, the rear wing is the most striking feature: it is a massive, flowing structure shaped like a curled cat tail or a magical ribbon, made of royal purple with gold edges. The wing has organic curves and appears to float slightly above the body, attached by invisible magnetic fields. On the rear flanks, small gold boomerang ornaments (Miniature boomerangs, each about 15 cm long) are mounted as decorative fins. The underbody glows with soft magenta light from the hover system.

Rear View: The tail of the car is rounded and perky. The taillights are two vertical, pink teardrops that glow softly. Between them, a fluffy, physical cat tail extends from the lower bumper — a real, fur‑covered tail with a white tip, wagging slightly (animated by magical energy). The rear wing arches high above, its shape reminiscent of a giant cat tail curling into a heart. The exhaust is a single, hidden outlet under the bumper, venting pink magical sparkles instead of smoke. The license plate is shaped like a heart, with “MOLINA” written in cute gold script.

Engine Detail (used in afterimage): The mid‑mounted “Magic Boomerang” engine is a rotary unit with a glowing pink housing. Inside, a transparent glass dome reveals a spinning magical core shaped like a tiny boomerang, glowing cyan. The engine cover has a cat paw print pattern.

Rear Wing (used in afterimage): A close‑up of the fantastical rear wing. It is made of a royal purple metallic material with a soft, velvet‑like texture. The wing has a curved, organic shape — like a ribbon or a tail — with gold trim along the edges. It is attached to the car by two thin, invisible magnetic stalks, making it appear to float. Small pink magical runes glow on its surface.

Wheels & Tires: Three‑spoke rims, each spoke shaped like a cat paw (rounded pads at the end). The rims are hot pink with gold centers. The tires are white‑walled with a pink pinstripe. Brake calipers are small, gold, and shaped like fish (playful detail).

Molina Spirit (used in afterimage): A translucent, ghost‑like creature floats beside the car, approximately 50 cm tall. It is a small, furry, round creature with large ears, a tiny face with big blue eyes, and a fluffy tail. Its body is made of soft cyan light with a warm glow. It has no legs — it floats like a magical familiar. It smiles gently. It is Molina, Nana’s deceased friend, now a protective spirit.

Magical Effects: Floating pink and cyan sparkles (magical dust) drift around the car. A soft, warm glow emanates from the underbody and the Molina spirit. Small, glowing purple flowers bloom briefly in the car’s wake (on the ground).

Lighting Consistency: All pink lighting (underbody glow, taillights, engine glow) shares a warm, candy pink hue — hex #FF69B4. The cyan lighting (Molina spirit, core, sparkles) is hex #00FFFF. The headlights are warm white with a hint of purple.

Not applicable — this is a full-bleed unified poster, not a multi-panel composition.

PANEL‑BY‑PANEL NARRATIVE, DIALOGUE AND SPEECH BUBBLES
Not applicable — no panels. The following describes the single unified composition from top to bottom.

The Canvas (9:16 vertical):
Upper Quarter — The Afterimage Array (Car Part Studies)

These afterimages are rendered semi‑transparent (approximately 40% opacity), floating in the magical forest air like enchanted holograms. Each afterimage is labeled with small, elegant calligraphic text integrated into the composition (sharp, high‑resolution, not hand‑drawn). Each afterimage shows a different part of the car in exquisite detail.

Afterimage 1 (far left) — Cat‑Ear Mirrors & Paw Grille:
A close‑up of the front: the two enormous fur‑covered cat‑ear mirrors, the smiling mouth grille with whisker LEDs, the round crystalline blue headlights, and the small pink nose emblem.

Middle‑Upper Background — Moonlit Enchanted Forest (Photorealistic Rendering)

Behind and between the afterimages, the background is a hyper‑realistic depiction of a moonlit enchanted forest — the Enchanted Forest of the Leonin. Towering ancient trees with silver bark and glowing purple leaves arch overhead. The ground is covered in soft moss and clusters of glowing mushrooms in shades of pink, cyan, and gold. Small fireflies (magical sprites) drift in the air, leaving trails of light. A massive, full moon hangs low in the sky, casting a silver‑blue light that creates long, soft shadows. A gentle mist floats at knee level, reflecting the moonlight. In the distance, a small wooden bridge crosses a stream of glowing water. The overall mood is dreamy, safe, and magical — a place of childhood wonder and bittersweet memory.

Lower Two‑Thirds — The Central Figure (Molina’s Gift Supercar)

The car is parked (hovering) in a small clearing, centered in the lower two‑thirds of the frame, angled slightly toward the viewer (three‑quarter front view from the right). The smiling cat face faces left, while the side profile reveals the magenta stripe, the retractable glass roof, the floating rear wing, and the fluffy tail. The pink underbody glow casts a warm reflection on the moss. The crystalline blue headlights are active, casting two soft beams forward that illuminate a cluster of glowing mushrooms. The Molina spirit floats beside the car, on the left side, glowing softly and smiling. Magical sparkles drift upward from the exhaust and the wheels. The rear wing arches high above the tail, its purple surface reflecting the moonlight.

The lighting is warm and magical: the moon provides a soft, cool backlight. The car’s pink underbody glow and the Molina spirit provide warm fill from below and side. The glowing mushrooms add scattered points of color. The result is a dreamy, high‑detail image that feels both cute and mystical.

The car shows natural imperfections: a tiny scuff on the front bumper (from a playful bump), a small leaf resting on the roof, a few dewdrops on the headlights, and a faint smudge on the paw print decal. These details reinforce the hyper‑realistic mandate.

Lower Quarter — Typography and Calligraphic Elements

Arranged as an integrated calligraphic composition over the lowest portion of the image, placed so it does not obscure the car’s wheels or the glowing mushrooms. The typography is rendered as sharp, high‑resolution text with subtle metallic textures and drop shadows, not hand‑drawn, but styled to evoke traditional Japanese seal and calligraphy forms (digitally perfected).

Hero’s Name (Car Model):
“MOLINA’S GIFT” lettered in large, semi‑calligraphic English text — thick‑and‑thin brush‑inspired strokes, rendered in hot pink with a soft gold outline. The letters are rounded, friendly, sweet.

Formal Title (below name):
“THE SWEET LEONIN’S MAGICAL RIDE” rendered slightly smaller, same calligraphic style but more restrained, soft white with lavender shadows.

Hanko Seal (below title):
An inked vermilion square seal, slightly imperfect edges as if hand‑carved, but rendered in photorealistic 4K. The seal contains a stylized boomerang crossed with a cat paw — Nana’s personal emblem adapted for the car.

Catchphrase (below seal):
“Every spell is a memory. Every memory is a gift.” Lettered in a smaller, elegant serif font with a slight italic tilt, faded silver with a pink glow.

Dialogue Quote (below catchphrase):
“Molina gave me his life so I could keep mine. Now I ride with him beside me — always.” Rendered in a slightly different typography — a clean, futuristic sans‑serif with a subtle metallic texture — to distinguish it as spoken or thematic text. Soft white with a faint cyan drop shadow.

Entire Image Mood: Sweet, nostalgic, magical, and undeniably cinematic. A main poster for a mystical leonin supercar — the kind that would be displayed in a child’s dream or a magical girl’s garage.

TITLE OF THE SERIES
WAVE OF THE WORLD — The Protector’s Kinetic Charger

CHAPTER / PIECE TITLE
Prologue — Volley Shot

IMAGE TYPE IDENTIFICATION
Single unified full-bleed 9:16 poster composition. No panels. No gutters. One continuous image with the visual and emotional force of a high‑energy, cyber‑athletic tapestry rendered in hyper‑realistic, cinematic photography.

ART STYLE DECLARATION — SACRED MANTRA
4K, 8K, HD, UHD, hyperdetailed, raw photo, cinematic lighting, anamorphic lens, sharp focus, intricate surface texture, subsurface scattering on translucent materials and energy effects, visible micro‑scratches and natural imperfections, film grain, color graded, ARRI Alexa look, 85mm lens, f/1.8 aperture, masterpiece quality.

The visual identity is that of a high‑budget cinematic advertisement for a futuristic one‑wheeled kinetic hover bike — every surface rendered with obsessive fidelity. The lighting is harsh and dramatic, with golden, sun‑baked highlights that define the bike’s bulky front armor and deep, dusty shadows that emphasize its aggressive stance. The color grading leans into a warm, athletic palette with charcoal grey, silver, cerulean blue, amber, and turquoise from the energy wheel. Film grain is present at 5% intensity, evenly distributed, adding texture without degrading the hyper‑detailed finish. The image must feel as though it was captured on a cinema camera with an anamorphic lens — slight oval bokeh in out‑of‑focus background elements, a subtle horizontal lens flare, and a shallow depth of field that keeps the bike razor‑sharp while the ancient arena behind it softens into atmospheric dust. Every material reads as real: forged carbon fiber, brushed titanium, armored plating, energy glass, and the faint heat shimmer from the exhaust.

The following elements are absolutely forbidden: 90s anime, cel animation, retro anime, VHS grain, scanlines, hand‑drawn line art, cel shading, pixelation, vector art, low resolution, flat colors, or any descriptor that suggests non‑photorealistic rendering.

CHARACTER REFERENCES — COMPLETE AND EXHAUSTIVE
Not applicable — no character appears. The one‑wheeled hover bike is the sole subject. The following design references are locked for all elements of the image.

Primary Bike — WAVE OF THE WORLD

Overall Silhouette: A one‑wheeled futuristic hover bike with a massively bulky front section (the “chest” and “shoulders”) and a low, rear‑mounted cockpit. The single wheel is a massive, glowing turquoise energy sphere, approximately 1.2 meters in diameter, located at the center of the chassis. The bike has no contact with the ground — it hovers approximately 30 cm above the surface using kinetic gyro thrusters integrated into the wheel hub and rear stabilizers. The front is armored and angular, like a cyber‑athlete’s padded chest and shoulder pads. The rear tapers into two vertical stabilizer fins and a single wide exhaust vent.

Color Palette: Charcoal grey (almost black) for the main armor. Silver for trim, hydraulic struts, and mechanical joints. Cerulean blue for the power core, accents, and energy lines. Amber for the visor strip and cockpit lighting. Turquoise for the energy wheel.

Front View (Bulky Head): The front is wide and aggressive, shaped like a cybernetic chest plate. Two large, curved “shoulder” plates extend outward, painted charcoal grey with silver edges. Between them, a central armored cowl houses a glowing amber visor strip — a single horizontal LED that acts as the “headlight,” mimicking Bruno’s tactical goggles. Below the visor, a narrow intake grille shaped like a soccer cleat’s studded sole (five horizontal slots). The entire front assembly is heavily armored, with visible rivets and layered plating.

Side Profile (Left Side): The massive turquoise energy wheel dominates the center. It is a solid sphere of translucent energy with a visible internal gyro ring that spins. The wheel does not touch the ground — it hovers, with a faint turquoise glow cast downward. The wheel is connected to the chassis by two thick, hydraulic “cyber‑leg” struts (one on each side), which are finished in silver with cerulean blue hydraulic lines. These struts mimic the look of Bruno’s mechanical thighs and knees, with exposed joints and circular blue vents at the pivot points. The front bulky armor extends from the wheel forward. Behind the wheel, a low, saddle‑like cockpit is recessed into the chassis. The cockpit has a curved, amber‑tinted canopy (empty). The rear of the bike features two vertical stabilizer fins painted charcoal grey with silver tips, and a single wide exhaust vent between them, shaped like a soccer cleat (rounded, with stud‑like ridges). The exhaust vents a faint blue‑white flame.

Rear View: The two vertical fins are parallel, each with a small red LED taillight on the trailing edge. The wide exhaust vent is visible between them, its cleat‑shaped ridges glowing faintly from heat. Below the exhaust, a small, circular anti‑gravity thruster (the rear stabilizer) glows turquoise. The underside of the bike is smooth, with no landing gear.

Engine & Wheel Detail (used in afterimage): The “Volley Shot” kinetic drive is housed inside the energy sphere. The sphere is composed of a translucent turquoise shell, inside which a fast‑spinning gyroscopic ring (silver) and a central cerulean blue power core are visible. Magnetic field lines are faintly visible as blue arcs. The wheel generates both hover and forward thrust through kinetic energy recovery and gyroscopic stabilization.

Cyber‑Leg Struts (used in afterimage): A close‑up of one of the hydraulic struts connecting the wheel to the chassis. It resembles a mechanical thigh: a thick silver cylinder with a cerulean blue hydraulic line running along the outer edge, terminating in a circular knee joint with exposed ball bearings and a glowing blue vent.

Cockpit & Visor (used in afterimage): A close‑up of the front cockpit area. The amber visor strip glows evenly. The canopy is an amber‑tinted, curved glass dome (empty). The cockpit interior shows a minimalist control grip with a single red “kick” button.

Rear Stabilizers & Exhaust (used in afterimage): A macro view of the tail section. The two vertical fins with red LED taillights, the wide cleat‑shaped exhaust vent venting blue‑white flame, and the small turquoise anti‑gravity thruster below.

Lighting Consistency: All amber lighting (visor strip, cockpit canopy tint) is hex #FFBF00. Turquoise lighting (energy wheel, rear thruster, internal gyro glow) is hex #00FFFF. Cerulean blue accents (hydraulic lines, power core, vents) are hex #2F6EFF. The exhaust flame is blue‑white (hex #E0FFFF).

Hover Effect: The bike has no wheels touching the ground. The energy sphere hovers 30 cm above the dusty arena floor, casting a soft turquoise glow downward. Dust particles are kicked up by the hover field, creating a circular clearing beneath the bike.

Kinetic Aura: Faint, translucent turquoise energy ribbons (kinetic trails) wrap around the bike’s frame, especially near the wheel and exhaust, suggesting stored momentum ready to be released.

PANEL‑BY‑PANEL NARRATIVE, DIALOGUE AND SPEECH BUBBLES
Not applicable — no panels. The following describes the single unified composition from top to bottom.

The Canvas (9:16 vertical):
Upper Quarter — The Afterimage Array (Bike Part Studies)

Filling the upper portion of the background, from approximately the top edge down to the main bike’s cockpit level, are four ghosted afterimages of the Wave of the World’s critical components. These afterimages are rendered semi‑transparent (approximately 40% opacity), floating in the dusty arena air like holographic tech schematics. Each afterimage is labeled with small, elegant calligraphic text integrated into the composition (sharp, high‑resolution, not hand‑drawn). Each afterimage shows a different part of the bike in exquisite detail.

Afterimage 1 (far left) — Bulky Front Armor & Amber Visor:
A close‑up of the front: the wide shoulder plates, the central cowl, the glowing amber visor strip, and the cleat‑pattern intake grille.
Label (calligraphic, small, floating below the afterimage): “BULKHEAD ARMOR — Reactive / Amber Visor LED Array”

Afterimage 2 (center‑left) — Energy Wheel & Gyro Core:
A macro view of the massive turquoise energy sphere, showing the translucent shell, the internal gyroscopic ring, the central cerulean blue power core, and magnetic field arcs.
Label: “VOLLEY SHOT KINETIC WHEEL — Gyro‑Stabilized / 1.2m Diameter”

Afterimage 3 (center‑right) — Cyber‑Leg Strut & Knee Joint:
A detailed view of one hydraulic strut: the silver cylinder, cerulean hydraulic line, circular knee joint with ball bearings, and the glowing blue vent.
Label: “CYBER‑LEG STRUT — Prosthetic Hydraulic / Kinetic Amplifier”

Afterimage 4 (far right) — Rear Stabilizers & Cleat Exhaust:
A close‑up of the tail section: the two vertical fins with red taillights, the wide cleat‑shaped exhaust vent with blue‑white flame, and the turquoise anti‑gravity thruster below.
Label: “STABILIZER FINS & CLEAT EXHAUST — Active Aero / Kinetic Venting”

All afterimages are positioned so they do not obstruct the central bike but instead frame it, creating a sense of high‑tech athletic engineering.

Middle‑Upper Background — Ancient Arena & Golden Sunlight (Photorealistic Rendering)

Behind and between the afterimages, the background is a hyper‑realistic depiction of an ancient arena under harsh, golden sunlight. Massive, weathered stone pillars with blue and gold geometric engravings line the edges of a circular arena floor. The ground is packed sand and dust, marked with faded grid lines and scuff marks from previous athletic contests. In the distance, the silhouette of a towering, technologically advanced city (Eruditio) rises beyond the arena walls, blending ancient and futuristic architecture. The sun is low but bright, casting long, sharp shadows and creating a warm, dusty haze. Dust particles float in the air, catching the golden light. The overall mood is one of intense competition, heroic legacy, and the fusion of sport and technology.

Lower Two‑Thirds — The Central Figure (Wave of the World Hover Bike)

The bike is centered in the lower two‑thirds of the frame, angled fifteen degrees toward the viewer (three‑quarter front view from the right). The bulky front armor faces left, while the side profile reveals the massive turquoise energy wheel, the cyber‑leg strut, the low cockpit, and the twin stabilizer fins. The amber visor strip is active, casting a narrow horizontal beam forward onto the dusty floor. The energy wheel glows turquoise, its internal gyro ring spinning (captured with slight motion blur). The exhaust vent emits a steady blue‑white flame that flickers. The bike hovers 30 cm above the ground, with a visible turquoise glow underneath and dust particles being pushed outward in a circular pattern. Faint turquoise kinetic energy ribbons swirl around the wheel and rear section.

The lighting is dramatic and warm: the golden sun from above‑right provides harsh key light, creating sharp highlights on the bulky front armor and the silver struts. Fill light comes from the bike’s own turquoise glow and the amber visor. The dust in the air catches the light, creating atmospheric depth. The result is a high‑contrast, action‑ready image that feels both powerful and agile.

The bike shows natural imperfections: a few scuff marks on the front armor (from simulated training impacts), dust accumulation on the silver struts, a tiny scratch on the amber visor, and a small heat‑discolored ring around the exhaust vent. These details reinforce the hyper‑realistic mandate.

Lower Quarter — Typography and Calligraphic Elements

Arranged as an integrated calligraphic composition over the lowest portion of the image, placed so it does not obscure the bike’s energy wheel or the ground glow. The typography is rendered as sharp, high‑resolution text with subtle metallic textures and drop shadows, not hand‑drawn, but styled to evoke traditional Japanese seal and calligraphy forms (digitally perfected).

Hero’s Name (Bike Model):
“WAVE OF THE WORLD” lettered in large, semi‑calligraphic English text — thick‑and‑thin brush‑inspired strokes, rendered in cerulean blue with a faint gold outline. The letters are spaced wide, dynamic, athletic.

Formal Title (below name):
“THE PROTECTOR’S KINETIC CHARGER” rendered slightly smaller, same calligraphic style but more restrained, soft silver with charcoal shadows.

Hanko Seal (below title):
An inked vermilion square seal, slightly imperfect edges as if hand‑carved, but rendered in photorealistic 4K. The seal contains a stylized soccer ball crossed with a cybernetic leg — Bruno’s personal emblem adapted for the bike.

Catchphrase (below seal):
“One kick can change the game. One game can change the world.” Lettered in a smaller, elegant serif font with a slight italic tilt, faded silver with a turquoise glow.

Dialogue Quote (below catchphrase):
“I was born frail. They gave me steel. Now I run so that others don’t have to fall.” Rendered in a slightly different typography — a clean, futuristic sans‑serif with a subtle metallic texture — to distinguish it as spoken or thematic text. Soft white with a faint amber drop shadow.

Entire Image Mood: Energetic, heroic, warm, and undeniably cinematic. A main poster for a futuristic one‑wheeled kinetic hover bike — the kind that would be displayed in a cyber‑athlete’s hall of fame.

TECHNICAL SPECIFICATIONS FOR IMAGE GENERATION
Aspect Ratio: 9:16 (vertical)

Art Style: Photorealistic cinematic rendering (as defined in Sacred Mantra)

Format: Single unified full-bleed composition — no panels, no gutters

Resolution: 8K (7680x13653 pixels equivalent), also compatible with 4K and HD downscales

Render Style: Hyper‑detailed raw photo simulation with anamorphic lens characteristics (oval bokeh, slight horizontal lens flare). ARRI Alexa 65 look.

Camera & Lens: 85mm prime lens at f/1.8, focused on the central bike. Shallow depth of field — background arena and afterimages slightly soft but legible.

Lighting: Warm, harsh, golden hour. Key light from sun (upper right). Fill light from bike’s turquoise wheel and amber visor. Rim light from dust reflections. The exhaust flame adds a cool blue accent.

Texture Requirements: Subsurface scattering on the energy wheel shell and the cockpit canopy. Visible brushed metal on armor panels, hydraulic lines with realistic rubber texture, dust particles on every surface, and faint heat shimmer above the exhaust. Tiny scuff marks and dust accumulation.

Film Grain: 5% intensity, 35mm film grain pattern overlay.

Color Grading: ARRI Look Library “Arena Champion” custom profile — warm gold highlights, cool turquoise accents, deep charcoal shadows. High contrast, medium brightness, rich blacks with detail.

Calligraphy & Typography Integration: Text elements are overlaid with subtle drop shadows and metallic textures, perfectly sharp. No hand‑drawn artifacts. The calligraphic forms are digitally rendered but inspired by seisho/reisho/gyosho/keisho brush aesthetics.

Aspect and Edge Finishing: A thin cerulean blue border (2% width) with a very faint inner gold glow. No deckled edges — clean, modern, cinematic.



TITLE OF THE SERIES
WEST JUSTICE — The Sheriff’s Armored Steed

CHAPTER / PIECE TITLE
Prologue — Quick Draw

IMAGE TYPE IDENTIFICATION
Single unified full-bleed 9:16 poster composition. No panels. No gutters. One continuous image with the visual and emotional force of a heroic, Western‑mythic tapestry rendered in hyper‑realistic, cinematic photography.

ART STYLE DECLARATION — SACRED MANTRA
4K, 8K, HD, UHD, hyperdetailed, raw photo, cinematic lighting, anamorphic lens, sharp focus, intricate surface texture, subsurface scattering on painted metal and leather, visible micro‑scratches and natural imperfections, film grain, color graded, ARRI Alexa look, 85mm lens, f/1.8 aperture, masterpiece quality.

The visual identity is that of a high‑budget cinematic advertisement for a legendary armored stallion — every surface rendered with obsessive fidelity. The lighting is warm and dramatic, with golden, dusty highlights that define the horse’s armor and deep, long shadows that emphasize its heroic, sturdy stance. The color grading leans into a rich Western palette with charcoal grey, tan leather, silver, brass, and the warm oranges of a desert sunset. Film grain is present at 5% intensity, evenly distributed, adding texture without degrading the hyper‑detailed finish. The image must feel as though it was captured on a cinema camera with an anamorphic lens — slight oval bokeh in out‑of‑focus background elements, a subtle horizontal lens flare, and a shallow depth of field that keeps the stallion razor‑sharp while the desert landscape behind it softens into atmospheric heat haze. Every material reads as real: polished silver, worn leather, brass cartridges, chainmail, and the faint scent of gunpowder implied by the weapons.

The following elements are absolutely forbidden: 90s anime, cel animation, retro anime, VHS grain, scanlines, hand‑drawn line art, cel shading, pixelation, vector art, low resolution, flat colors, or any descriptor that suggests non‑photorealistic rendering.

CHARACTER REFERENCES — COMPLETE AND EXHAUSTIVE
Not applicable — no character appears. The armored stallion is the sole subject. The following design references are locked for all elements of the image.

Primary Subject — ARMORED STALLION (WEST JUSTICE)

Overall Silhouette: A massive, sturdy stallion with a broad chest, powerful shoulders, and thick, muscular legs. The horse stands tall and proud, exuding heroic presence. It is fully armored in a Western/cowboy style — a fusion of traditional horse barding with frontier gunslinger aesthetics. The armor is a mix of silver‑plated studded leather, chainmail, and fitted metal plates. The horse’s posture is alert, head high, ears forward, one front hoof slightly raised as if ready to ride into action.

Color Palette: Dark charcoal grey for the horse’s natural coat (visible on the muzzle, lower legs, and under the armor). Tan leather for the barding, saddle, and holsters. Silver for metal plates, studs, and the sheriff star. Brass for bullet casings and fittings. Deep crimson for the saddle blanket and bandana accents. Piercing blue‑grey for the horse’s glowing eyes.

Head & Face: The horse’s head is protected by a fitted metal “hat” shaped like a wide‑brimmed cowboy hat — actually a helmet that covers the skull, with cutouts for the ears. The brim is silver, curved upward at the sides, with a distinct silver V‑shaped emblem on the crown. A small tan feather is tucked into the band (metal sculpted to look like a feather). The horse’s eyes are visible through two almond‑shaped cutouts, glowing with a soft, piercing blue‑grey light. The muzzle is uncovered, showing the horse’s dark grey coat, a soft pink nostril, and a silver bit in the mouth attached to braided leather reins. The ears are upright, each wearing a small silver earring (a bullet-shaped charm).

Neck & Chest: The neck is protected by overlapping silver‑plated scales (like a gorget) that extend from the jaw to the shoulders. Below the scales, a thick crimson bandana is tied around the base of the neck, its ends trailing in the wind. The chest is broad and heavily armored with a silver breastplate that features a large, six‑pointed sheriff star (polished silver) at the center. The breastplate is edged with brass studs. From the breastplate, two leather bandoliers cross the chest, each holding rows of brass rifle cartridges.

Body & Saddle Area: The horse’s back is covered by a fitted tan leather barding (armored blanket) that extends from the shoulders to the hindquarters. The leather is tooled with Western floral patterns and studded with silver conchos. Over this rests a deep crimson saddle blanket. The saddle itself is a classic Western design, made of dark brown leather with silver horn, stirrups, and conchos. On each side of the saddle, a leather rifle scabbard is strapped, holding a long‑barreled repeater rifle (wood stock, silver receiver). Additionally, two short revolving cannons (cut‑down shotguns or revolvers) are holstered on the front of the saddle, one on each side, with brass cylinders visible. A coiled lariat (rope) hangs from the saddle horn.

Flanks & Hindquarters: The horse’s flanks are covered by fringed leather panels that hang from the saddle, decorated with brass bullet tips along the edges. Over the hindquarters, a metal crupper (tail armor) protects the rump, shaped like a shield with a silver star. The tail itself is natural but wrapped with leather bands and small silver rings.

Legs & Hooves: All four legs are armored from knee/hock down with silver‑plated greaves, each greave having a row of brass studs. The hooves are steel‑shod, with a small silver rowel (spur wheel) attached to the back of each hoof — not for riding but as a decorative and functional element (the horse can kick with spurs). The hooves have a subtle glow from the steel.

Weapons & Accessories (Detailed):

Bandoliers: Two cross‑chest bandoliers, each with 12 brass cartridges (visible bullet tips).

Rifle scabbards (left and right): Each holds a lever‑action repeater with a dark wood stock, polished silver receiver, and long octagonal barrel.

Revolving cannons (front saddle holsters): Short, large‑bore revolvers with brass cylinder and hammer, engraved with Western scrollwork.

Saddle rings: Small loops for additional ammunition.

Bit and reins: Silver bit, leather reins with silver conchos.

Lighting & Glow Effects: The horse’s eyes glow with a soft, steady blue‑grey light (hex #A0C4E2). The silver armor and brass cartridges reflect the orange sunset. No magical aura — the glow is a subtle cybernetic or heroic enhancement, not fantasy.

Stance & Environment Integration: The horse stands on a dusty desert road, head slightly turned toward the viewer, one front hoof lifted. Its shadow stretches long to the right. Dust swirls around its hooves.

The silver sheriff star catches the sunset, gleaming like a beacon. The crimson bandana flutters slightly in a dry breeze. The rifles in their scabbards are visible, their wood stocks smooth and polished. The brass cartridges on the bandoliers reflect the orange light. The steel‑shod hooves rest on the dusty ground, with one front hoof slightly raised. Dust swirls around the hooves, kicked up by an invisible wind.

The lighting is dramatic and golden: the setting sun from behind‑left provides warm backlighting, creating a silhouette effect on the horse’s right side. Fill light comes from the glowing eyes and the reflections off the silver armor. The result is a high‑contrast, heroic image that feels both grounded and legendary.

The stallion shows natural imperfections: a few scratches on the silver breastplate (from old battles), dust settled on the leather saddle, a small scuff on the rifle stock, a single strand of mane caught on the bandolier. The horse’s coat has natural muscle definition and a few stray hairs. These details reinforce the hyper‑realistic mandate.

Hero’s Name (Stallion’s Title):

TITLE OF THE SERIES
MYSTIC GUSH — The Professor’s Arcane Vessel

CHAPTER / PIECE TITLE
Prologue — The Forbidden Experiment

IMAGE TYPE IDENTIFICATION
Single unified full-bleed 9:16 poster composition. No panels. No gutters. One continuous image with the visual and emotional force of a cosmic, arcane tapestry rendered in hyper‑realistic, cinematic photography.

ART STYLE DECLARATION — SACRED MANTRA
4K, 8K, HD, UHD, hyperdetailed, raw photo, cinematic lighting, anamorphic lens, sharp focus, intricate surface texture, subsurface scattering on translucent energy surfaces and plasma, visible micro‑scratches and natural imperfections, film grain, color graded, ARRI Alexa look, 85mm lens, f/1.8 aperture, masterpiece quality.

The visual identity is that of a high‑budget cinematic advertisement for a futuristic one‑man mystic spaceship — every surface rendered with obsessive fidelity. The lighting is dramatic and electric, with cold, cyan highlights that define the ship’s angular hull and deep, purple‑black shadows that emphasize its arcane, cosmic origin. The color grading leans into a celestial, high‑contrast palette with deep violet, obsidian black, glowing cyan, white, and hints of gold and purple from the nebula background. Film grain is present at 5% intensity, evenly distributed, adding texture without degrading the hyper‑detailed finish. Chair Frame (Wood & Brass): The frame is carved mahogany with intricate Victorian scrollwork. The armrests are wide and curved, each featuring an exposed brass gear set (three interlocking gears) that turn slowly when the chair is active. The legs are short, ending in brass claw‑and‑ball feet, but they do not touch the ground — the chair floats.

Floating Orbs (Hover System): Four spherical brass orbs are positioned under the chair — one near each corner. Each orb is approximately 15 cm in diameter, made of polished brass with visible seam lines and a glowing emerald green core. The orbs spin slowly (captured with slight motion blur), and they emit a soft green glow that casts light upward onto the underside of the chair. Small emerald sparks occasionally fall from the orbs.

Floating Clock Hands: Surrounding the chair are three floating golden clock hands: an hour hand (short, wide, ornate), a minute hand (long, slender, pointed), and a second hand (very thin, with a counterweight). They are not attached to any mechanism — they drift slowly in the air, rotating around the chair at different speeds. Each hand has intricate engravings and a soft golden glow at the tip.

Armrest Details (Clockwork Gears): On each armrest, a small cutout reveals a set of three meshing brass gears (different sizes). The gears turn slowly, powered by unseen magic. Behind the gears, a faint emerald glow pulses.

Card Slots & Magic Cards: On the outer side of each armrest, there are two thin slots (like card holders) from which glowing magical playing cards protrude. The cards are translucent, with red spade symbols and a soft ruby glow. There are four visible cards total (two on each side). The cards appear to be made of solidified light.

Headrest Gemstone: At the top center of the backrest, a large, faceted ruby red gemstone is set into a brass housing. The gem pulses with a slow, rhythmic light (like a heartbeat). Small red sparks occasionally jump from its facets.

Underside & Hover Effect: The underside of the chair is smooth mahogany with brass inlays. The four floating orbs are visible, each casting a distinct emerald glow onto the floor below. The chair leaves no marks — it floats silently.

Magical Effects: Faint emerald green and ruby red particles drift upward from the chair, like magical dust. The floating clock hands cast soft golden reflections on the wood. The air around the chair shimmers with a barely visible arcane heat haze.

Lighting Consistency: All emerald green lighting (orbs, particle glow) is hex #00FF88. The ruby red gem and card symbols are hex #FF0044. The floating clock hands glow soft gold (hex #FFD700). The overall magical aura is a mix of green and red.

TITLE OF THE SERIES

IMAGE TYPE IDENTIFICATION
