TECHNICAL SPECIFICATIONS FOR IMAGE GENERATION
Aspect Ratio: 9:16 (vertical)

Art Style: Photorealistic cinematic rendering (as defined in Sacred Mantra)

Format: Single unified full-bleed composition — no panels, no gutters

Resolution: 8K (7680x13653 pixels equivalent), also compatible with 4K and HD downscales

Render Style: Hyper‑detailed raw photo simulation with anamorphic lens characteristics (oval bokeh, slight horizontal lens flare). ARRI Alexa 65 look.

Camera & Lens: 85mm prime lens at f/1.8, focused on the central chariot and lead fallen angel. Shallow depth of field — background vortex and afterimages slightly soft but legible.

Lighting: Infernal, dramatic, high‑contrast. Key light from angels’ green visors and chariot’s green crystals (acidic green). Fill light from abyssal vortex (purple/violet). Rim light from crimson chains and shadow flames. Strong contrast between cool greens, warm reds, and deep purples.

Texture Requirements: Subsurface scattering on the green visor glow, the crystal pulses, and the shadow flames. Visible forged metal on chariot, tattered fabric on robes, feather texture with missing barbs, chainmail links, stone cracks, and rune glows. Floating feathers and ash particles.

Film Grain: 5% intensity, 35mm film grain pattern overlay.

Color Grading: ARRI Look Library “Fallen Throne” custom profile — acidic green highlights, bruised purple midtones, deep charcoal shadows, crimson accents. High contrast, medium brightness, rich dark blacks.

Calligraphy & Typography Integration: Text elements are overlaid with subtle drop shadows and metallic textures, perfectly sharp. No hand‑drawn artifacts. The calligraphic forms are digitally rendered but inspired by seisho/reisho/gyosho/keisho brush aesthetics.

Aspect and Edge Finishing: A thin silver border (2% width) with a very faint inner green glow. No deckled edges — clean, modern, cinematic.







TITLE OF THE SERIES
ULTIMATE SNIPE — The Sniper Princess’s Ballistic Steed

CHAPTER / PIECE TITLE
Prologue — Lethal Shot

IMAGE TYPE IDENTIFICATION
Single unified full-bleed 9:16 poster composition. No panels. No gutters. One continuous image with the visual and emotional force of a high‑tech, precision‑engineered sniper bike tapestry rendered in hyper‑realistic, cinematic photography.

ART STYLE DECLARATION — SACRED MANTRA
4K, 8K, HD, UHD, hyperdetailed, raw photo, cinematic lighting, anamorphic lens, sharp focus, intricate surface texture, subsurface scattering on translucent materials, holographic displays, and glowing energy, visible micro‑scratches and natural imperfections, film grain, color graded, ARRI Alexa look, 85mm lens, f/1.8 aperture, masterpiece quality.

The visual identity is that of a high‑budget cinematic advertisement for a futuristic sniper sports bike — every surface rendered with obsessive fidelity. The lighting is crisp and golden, with warm sunrise highlights that define the bike’s matte curves and cool, teal reflections from its holographic display and energy accents. Deep, clean shadows emphasize its sleek, lethal precision. The color grading leans into a noble, tactical palette with matte black, royal blue, glowing teal, silver, and warm gold from the sunrise. Film grain is present at 5% intensity, evenly distributed, adding texture without degrading the hyper‑detailed finish. The image must feel as though it was captured on a cinema camera with an anamorphic lens — slight oval bokeh in out‑of‑focus background elements, a subtle horizontal lens flare, and a shallow depth of field that keeps the bike razor‑sharp while the white marble plaza and distant spires behind it soften into atmospheric beauty. Every material reads as real: carbon fiber, brushed aluminum, optical glass, rubber, and the faint scent of ozone and gun oil.

The following elements are absolutely forbidden: 90s anime, cel animation, retro anime, VHS grain, scanlines, hand‑drawn line art, cel shading, pixelation, vector art, low resolution, flat colors, or any descriptor that suggests non‑photorealistic rendering.

CHARACTER REFERENCES — COMPLETE AND EXHAUSTIVE
Not applicable — no character appears. The sports bike is the sole subject. The following design references are locked for all elements of the image.

Primary Bike — ULTIMATE SNIPE (Ballistic Steed)

Overall Silhouette: A sleek, matte‑black and royal blue super sports bike with a long, low, aggressive profile. The front is narrow and pointed, with a single piercing teal LED headlight on the left side. The right side of the front fairing has a smooth, black cover where a second headlight would be – a deliberate asymmetric detail mimicking Lesley’s eyepatch. The bike has a streamlined fuel tank with royal blue accents, a low rear seat hump, and twin exhausts at the rear venting silver‑blue flame. An integrated sniper rifle is mounted flush along the right side of the front fairing, with a long silver barrel extending just past the front wheel and a glowing teal scope. Above the cockpit area, a holographic heads‑up display (HUD) projects translucent teal and gold targeting data, ballistic trajectories, and a crosshair reticle, visible in mid‑air. The wheels have five thin spokes, glowing teal rim LEDs, and low‑profile tires. The bike is approximately 2.1 meters long, 0.7 meters wide, and sits on the ground (no hover, but low stance).

Color Palette: Matte black (primary body, fairing, tank). Royal blue (accents, pinstripes, seat hump). Glowing teal (headlight, scope, HUD, rim LEDs, exhaust flame). Silver (rifle barrel, exhaust tips, wheel rims). Warm gold (HUD targeting accents, sunrise reflection).

Front View: The front fairing is asymmetrical. On the left side, a single, round, piercing teal LED headlight glows with a cool, bright light. On the right side, a smooth, matte black panel covers the area where a second headlight would be, with a subtle silver edge – the “eyepatch” cover. The fairing nose is pointed, with a small, dark windshield. Below, the integrated sniper rifle is visible: a silver barrel protruding from the right side of the fairing, with a teal LED ring at the muzzle and a compact scope mounted on top, glowing teal. The front fender is split, painted matte black with a royal blue stripe.

Side Profile (Left Side): The left profile shows the clean, uninterrupted body. The fuel tank is sculpted with sharp creases, matte black with a royal blue pinstripe. The seat is a low, single‑piece hump (no passenger accommodation). The swingarm is brushed aluminum. The rear has twin exhaust pipes (one on each side), silver with teal tips, venting a faint silver‑blue flame. The left side has no rifle, keeping the silhouette clean.

Side Profile (Right Side): The right profile reveals the integrated sniper rifle. The rifle is mounted flush along the fairing, with a slim, matte silver barrel extending forward just past the front wheel axis. The scope is a compact, cylindrical unit with a glowing teal eyepiece and a gold lens flare. The rifle has a small, teal‑glowing ammunition counter on its side. The bike’s controls include clip‑on handlebars with black grips and a small control panel on the right handlebar with a red trigger button.

Rear View: The tail tapers into a sharp point, with a thin, horizontal teal LED taillight. The twin exhausts are positioned symmetrically, each with a silver tip and a flickering silver‑blue flame. The rear tire is wide, with a teal pinstripe. The license plate bracket is small and recessed, shaped like a bullet.

Holographic HUD (used in afterimage): Projected in front of the rider’s position (empty) is a translucent, glowing heads‑up display. It consists of a circular targeting reticle (teal with a gold center crosshair), range finder numbers (e.g., “450m”), wind speed (”2.1 m/s”), and a ballistic drop line. The HUD is semi‑transparent, floating in the air, with a faint scan line effect. It casts a soft teal glow on the bike’s tank.

Integrated Sniper Rifle (used in afterimage): A macro view of the rifle. The barrel is silver, with a teal LED ring at the muzzle. The scope is a matte black cylinder with a glowing teal front lens and a gold lens flare. The side of the rifle has a small digital ammo counter reading “4” (four bullets) in teal. The rifle is seamlessly integrated into the fairing, with no visible screws.

Single Headlight & Eyepatch Cover (used in afterimage): A close‑up of the front: the left, glowing teal headlight; the right, smooth black cover with a silver edge (eyepatch motif). The cover has a faint, subtle royal blue outline.

Wheel & Exhaust (used in afterimage): A macro view of the rear wheel: five thin silver spokes, a glowing teal rim LED strip, and a low‑profile tire. Adjacent, the twin exhaust tip with silver‑blue flame.

Lighting & Effects: The teal headlight casts a narrow, focused beam. The HUD projects translucent light, with faint particles. The exhaust flame is a cool silver‑blue, with a slight heat shimmer. The bike’s reflection is visible in the polished marble floor.

Aura & Atmosphere: A faint, cool teal energy aura surrounds the bike, with tiny digital pixels floating from the HUD. The bike sits on a pristine white marble plaza under a golden sunrise.

Background Setting: A celestial citadel at dawn – white marble spires, golden buttresses, expansive plazas with polished stone, and towering sword‑like pillars draped with royal blue banners. The sky is a warm gradient from pale gold near the horizon to soft lavender above.

TITLE OF THE SERIES
DOMINATOR’S DESCENT — The Planes Dominator’s Nightmare Vessel

CHAPTER / PIECE TITLE
Prologue — Nightmaric Spawn

IMAGE TYPE IDENTIFICATION
Single unified full-bleed 9:16 poster composition. No panels. No gutters. One continuous image with the visual and emotional force of a cosmic, biomechanical, evil alien warship tapestry rendered in hyper‑realistic, cinematic photography.

ART STYLE DECLARATION — SACRED MANTRA
4K, 8K, HD, UHD, hyperdetailed, raw photo, cinematic lighting, anamorphic lens, sharp focus, intricate surface texture, subsurface scattering on translucent organic components and glowing energy, visible micro‑scratches and natural imperfections, film grain, color graded, ARRI Alexa look, 85mm lens, f/1.8 aperture, masterpiece quality.

The visual identity is that of a high‑budget cinematic advertisement for a colossal, biomechanical alien warship — every surface rendered with obsessive fidelity. The lighting is dramatic and oppressive, with intense crimson and orange highlights that define the ship’s jagged edges and deep, abyssal shadows that emphasize its massive, terrifying presence. The color grading leans into an alien, predatory palette with iridescent purple, silver chitin, glowing crimson, deep indigo, and burning orange. Film grain is present at 5% intensity, evenly distributed, adding texture without degrading the hyper‑detailed finish. The image must feel as though it was captured on a cinema camera with an anamorphic lens — slight oval bokeh in out‑of‑focus background elements, a subtle horizontal lens flare, and a shallow depth of field that keeps the ship razor‑sharp while the twilight battlefield behind it softens into atmospheric desolation. Every material reads as real: chitinous armor, organic sinew, pulsating conduits, alien metal, and the faint hum of reality being torn apart.

The following elements are absolutely forbidden: 90s anime, cel animation, retro anime, VHS grain, scanlines, hand‑drawn line art, cel shading, pixelation, vector art, low resolution, flat colors, or any descriptor that suggests non‑photorealistic rendering.

CHARACTER REFERENCES — COMPLETE AND EXHAUSTIVE
Not applicable — no character appears. The spaceship is the sole subject. The following design references are locked for all elements of the image.

Primary Ship — NIGHTMARE VESSEL (Dominator’s Descent)

Overall Silhouette: A colossal, biomechanical alien warship shaped like a giant, four‑winged insectoid predator. The hull is asymmetrical, with a broad, flattened central body and two pairs of sweeping, jagged wings (upper and lower) that curve backward like those of a demonic dragonfly. The ship is approximately 300 meters long (implied scale), with a massive central maw on the underside that glows with burning orange energy. Four segmented, scorpion‑like tail cannons extend from the rear, each curved and tipped with a glowing crimson sensor orb. The ship hovers approximately 100 meters above the ground, casting an oppressive shadow over the battlefield. The surface is covered in iridescent purple and silver chitin armor plates, with pulsating organic conduits (glowing orange and crimson) visible in the gaps. Dozens of smaller glowing turrets dot the hull.

Color Palette: Iridescent purple (primary hull). Silver chitin (armor edges, wing tips). Glowing crimson (sensor eyes, conduits, turret tips). Burning orange (central maw, engine glow, weapon cores). Deep indigo (shadowed undersides). Black and dark purple (abyssal sky).

Central Hull & Wings: The main body is a flattened, organic shape like a stingray or beetle, with a raised spinal ridge. Two massive upper wings sweep back and up, each with a serrated trailing edge and a sharp, silver‑tipped point. Two smaller lower wings angle downward. The wings have visible vein-like structures (organic conduits) glowing faintly orange. The hull is covered in overlapping chitin plates with a pearl-like, iridescent sheen that shifts from purple to silver under light.

Central Maw (Underside): On the belly of the ship, a massive, vertical slit (the “maw”) glows with intense, pulsating orange energy. The edges of the maw are lined with inward‑curving teeth-like spikes (silver chitin). From within, a swirling vortex of orange plasma and black smoke is visible. The maw is the ship’s primary weapon or assimilation port.

Tail Cannons: Four segmented, scorpion‑like tails extend from the rear of the hull, each approximately 50 meters long. Each tail has multiple articulated segments (like a spine), ending in a curved, sharp tip that houses a glowing crimson orb. The orb pulses slowly, and small arcs of crimson energy jump between the tails.

Organic Conduits & Pulsing Veins: Across the hull, thick, organic tubes (resembling blood vessels or cables) run between armor plates. They glow with a slow, rhythmic orange or crimson light, like a heartbeat. Some conduits are exposed, with small droplets of glowing fluid visible.

Turret Arrays: Dozens of small, dome‑shaped turrets are embedded in the hull. Each turret has a single glowing crimson eye (laser lens) and can rotate. In the poster, they are idle, but their eyes glow steadily.

Lighting & Glow Effects: The central maw emits a warm, pulsing orange glow that illuminates the underside of the ship. The tail orbs glow steady crimson. The conduits pulse in sequence. The ship’s overall silhouette is backlit by a dying sun.

Aura & Atmosphere: The ship is surrounded by a faint, shimmering heat haze and small, floating embers (orange and black). The air around it seems to distort. A faint, subsonic hum is implied.

Background Setting: A desolate, twilight battlefield. Cracked earth with sparse patches of glowing blue flora. Jagged, monolithic mountains in the distance. The sky is a deep indigo with a dying red sun on the horizon and faint stars. Floating ruins and celestial debris hang suspended in the air. A thin, reddish‑purple fog covers the ground.

Jagged, monolithic mountains rise in the distance, their peaks sharp against a dark indigo sky. The sun is a dying red orb on the horizon, casting a faint, blood‑colored glow. Floating ruins (shattered pillars, broken arches) and celestial debris drift suspended in the air, caught in the planet’s weak gravity. A thin, reddish‑purple fog hugs the ground. The atmosphere is heavy, silent, and oppressive — a world already conquered.

Lower Two‑Thirds — The Central Figure (Nightmare Vessel Spaceship)

The massive spaceship dominates the lower two‑thirds of the frame, angled slightly upward, looming over the viewer. The four wings are spread wide, the central maw glows bright orange, and the tail cannons curl menacingly. The ship’s underside is visible, with the maw’s teeth spikes and swirling vortex. The crimson tail orbs pulse. The organic conduits across the hull glow in slow waves. Dozens of turret eyes glow faintly. The ship casts a massive, dark shadow over the cracked earth below, where the blue flora seems to dim in its presence.

The lighting is dramatic and hellish: the dying red sun provides a dim, blood‑colored backlight, silhouetting the ship’s upper edges. The ship’s own orange maw and crimson conduits provide intense, warm key light from below and within. The blue flora adds a cool, alien accent. The result is a high‑contrast, terrifying image that feels like the arrival of an unstoppable predator.

The ship shows natural imperfections: a few small scratches on the chitin plates, a tiny leak from one conduit (a glowing droplet falling), a scorch mark near a turret base, and a single loose armor plate on the lower wing. These details reinforce the hyper‑realistic mandate.

Lower Quarter — Typography and Calligraphic Elements

Arranged as an integrated calligraphic composition over the lowest portion of the image, placed so it does not obscure the ship’s shadow or the blue flora. The typography is rendered as sharp, high‑resolution text with subtle metallic textures and drop shadows, not hand‑drawn, but styled to evoke traditional Japanese seal and calligraphy forms (digitally perfected).

Hero’s Name (Ship Name):
“DOMINATOR’S DESCENT” lettered in large, semi‑calligraphic English text — thick‑and‑thin brush‑inspired strokes, rendered in burning orange with a faint crimson outline. The letters are spaced wide, oppressive, absolute.

Formal Title (below name):
“THE PLANES DOMINATOR’S NIGHTMARE VESSEL” rendered slightly smaller, same calligraphic style but more restrained, soft silver with deep indigo shadows.

Hanko Seal (below title):
An inked vermilion square seal, slightly imperfect edges as if hand‑carved, but rendered in photorealistic 4K. The seal contains a stylized alien skull crossed with a scorpion tail — Zhask’s personal emblem adapted for the ship.

Catchphrase (below seal):
“The sky is silent. The planet is screaming.” Lettered in a smaller, elegant serif font with a slight italic tilt, faded silver with an orange glow.

Dialogue Quote (below catchphrase):
“I have consumed worlds older than your sun. This continent is just a appetizer.” Rendered in a slightly different typography — a clean, futuristic sans‑serif with a subtle metallic texture — to distinguish it as spoken or thematic text. Soft white with a faint crimson drop shadow.

Entire Image Mood: Oppressive, terrifying, cosmic, and undeniably cinematic. A main poster for an alien conqueror’s warship — the kind that would be displayed in a galactic empire’s hall of conquest.

TECHNICAL SPECIFICATIONS FOR IMAGE GENERATION
Aspect Ratio: 9:16 (vertical)

Art Style: Photorealistic cinematic rendering (as defined in Sacred Mantra)

Format: Single unified full-bleed composition — no panels, no gutters

Resolution: 8K (7680x13653 pixels equivalent), also compatible with 4K and HD downscales

TITLE OF THE SERIES
ETERNAL GUARD — The Shimmer of Hope’s Necrokeep Chariot

CHAPTER / PIECE TITLE
Prologue — Deathly Grasp

IMAGE TYPE IDENTIFICATION
Single unified full-bleed 9:16 poster composition. No panels. No gutters. One continuous image with the visual and emotional force of a spectral, regal, necromantic tapestry rendered in hyper‑realistic, cinematic photography.

ART STYLE DECLARATION — SACRED MANTRA
4K, 8K, HD, UHD, hyperdetailed, raw photo, cinematic lighting, anamorphic lens, sharp focus, intricate surface texture, subsurface scattering on translucent materials, ghostly mist, and glowing crystals, visible micro‑scratches and natural imperfections, film grain, color graded, ARRI Alexa look, 85mm lens, f/1.8 aperture, masterpiece quality.

The visual identity is that of a high‑budget cinematic advertisement for a ghostly necromantic chariot — every surface rendered with obsessive fidelity. The lighting is dramatic and spectral, with cool teal and pale green highlights that define the chariot’s ornate edges and deep, abyssal shadows that emphasize its undead, royal presence. The color grading leans into a haunted, regal palette with obsidian black, aged gold, glowing teal, pale ghostly grey, and soft violet from the twilight sky. Film grain is present at 5% intensity, evenly distributed, adding texture without degrading the hyper‑detailed finish. The image must feel as though it was captured on a cinema camera with an anamorphic lens — slight oval bokeh in out‑of‑focus background elements, a subtle horizontal lens flare, and a shallow depth of field that keeps the chariot and wraiths razor‑sharp while the desolate mountain pass behind them softens into atmospheric mist. Every material reads as real: carved obsidian, tarnished gold, velvet, spectral energy, and the faint scent of grave dust and incense.

The following elements are absolutely forbidden: 90s anime, cel animation, retro anime, VHS grain, scanlines, hand‑drawn line art, cel shading, pixelation, vector art, low resolution, flat colors, or any descriptor that suggests non‑photorealistic rendering.

CHARACTER REFERENCES — COMPLETE AND EXHAUSTIVE
Not applicable — no character appears. The chariot and three faceless wraiths are the sole subjects. The following design references are locked for all elements of the image.

Primary Subject 1 — Necrokeep Chariot (Cursed Throne on Wheels)

Overall Silhouette: A majestic, spectral chariot shaped like a cursed throne room on two large, spoked wheels. The chariot body is an open platform with a high‑backed throne at the center, facing forward. The frame is made of obsidian black metal with aged gold filigree. The wheels are large (1.8 meters in diameter), each with eight curved, skeletal‑looking spokes ending in small spikes, rims wreathed in ghostly grey‑green mist. The chariot is draped with tattered velvet curtains in deep purple and crimson, hanging from a canopy frame. Glowing teal crystals are embedded in the throne and canopy, pulsing slowly. The chariot hovers approximately 50 cm above the ground, surrounded by a faint, cold mist.

Color Palette: Obsidian black (primary frame, throne). Aged gold (trim, spokes, crown motifs). Glowing teal (crystals, runes, spectral accents). Deep purple and crimson (tattered velvet drapes). Pale ghostly grey (mist). Silver (chain links).

Chariot Body & Throne: The platform is an asymmetrical, jagged shape of black metal. The throne is high‑backed, with armrests ending in skeletal claw grips. The backrest features a crown‑shaped crest with a central teal crystal. The seat is upholstered in dark velvet. A small canopy frame arches over the throne, from which tattered velvet curtains hang, some torn, some floating as if in a spectral wind. Glowing teal runes (fictional) are etched into the throne’s sides.

Wheels & Mist: Two massive wheels at the rear. Each spoke is thin, curved like a rib bone, with a small spike at the tip. The rims are gold. The wheels do not touch the ground — they hover, with ghostly grey‑green mist swirling around the spokes and rising from the axle hubs. The mist is semi‑transparent, with tiny pale sparks.

Harness & Chains: From the front yoke, three chains of pale spectral light (silver‑white with a faint teal glow) extend to the three wraiths. The chains have no physical links — they are solidified energy, with a soft, ethereal shimmer.

Hover Effect: The entire chariot floats above the ground, with a cold mist pooling underneath. The wheels spin slowly in place, but the chariot is stationary in the composition.

Primary Subject 2 — Three Faceless Mist‑Wraiths (Bound Pullers)

Overall Silhouette: Three tall, gaunt humanoid figures, each approximately 2.8 meters tall, hovering just above the ground, leaning forward into the pull. They have no faces — only smooth, featureless oval heads of pale grey, like weathered stone or wax. Their bodies are wrapped in tattered, dark grey robes that dissolve into swirling mist at the lower half (no legs or feet). Their arms are long and thin, ending in bony, three‑fingered hands that grip the spectral chains. They have no visible eyes, mouths, or ears — only a subtle, sad tilt to their heads.

Color Palette: Pale grey (head, hands). Dark charcoal and deep grey (robes). Ghostly white‑teal (mist lower body). Silver‑white (energy chains).

Head & Face: The head is smooth, featureless, slightly elongated, like a porcelain mask with no carved features. The surface has a matte, stone‑like texture, with faint, hairline cracks. No eyes, no nose, no mouth. A faint, cold aura emanates from the crown.

Robes & Mist Lower Body: The wraiths wear hooded robes of tattered, dark grey fabric, with frayed edges and small tears. The hood is pulled up, but the face remains visible. From the waist down, the robes transition into swirling, semi‑transparent mist of grey‑white with pale teal undertones. The mist billows and trails behind them, leaving no footprints. Small, cold embers (white) drift from the mist.

Arms & Hands: The arms are unnaturally long, with bony, angular elbows. Hands have three long fingers and an opposable thumb, all ending in sharp, grey nails. They clasp the spectral chains with a firm, undead grip.

Aura & Effects: Each wraith is surrounded by a faint, cold mist. Their heads emit a subtle, sad luminescence. Small, pale sparks fall from their hands. The mist lower bodies swirl constantly.

Background Setting: A desolate, high‑altitude mountain pass under a twilight sky. Jagged cliffs and floating rock formations glow faintly with teal energy. A winding, ethereal path of violet light snakes through the valley below. Distant ruins of a gothic fortress on a peak. The sky is deep indigo with faint stars and wispy lavender clouds. Glowing blue flowers (mountain moss) dot the ground.

A thin, cold mist hugs the earth. The overall mood is one of silent, undying sorrow — the domain of a cursed queen.

Lower Two‑Thirds — The Central Figures (Chariot + Three Wraiths)

The ensemble is captured in mid‑motion, moving from left to right across the frame. The three faceless wraiths are in the foreground, side‑by‑side in a staggered formation (one slightly ahead, two behind), leaning forward against the spectral chains. Their featureless heads are tilted slightly downward, their hands gripping the chains. Their lower bodies dissolve into swirling mist that trails behind. The chariot floats behind them, approximately three meters back. The high throne is empty, facing forward. The two skeletal wheels hover, ghostly mist swirling around their spokes. The teal crystals on the throne pulse slowly. The tattered velvet curtains sway gently in an unfelt wind. A cold, pale glow emanates from the throne’s crystal, casting a soft teal light on the wraiths.

The lighting is dramatic and spectral: the twilight sky provides a dim, cool backlight in deep blue and violet. The chariot’s teal crystals and the wraiths’ pale aura provide cool, eerie key light from the front. The glowing blue flowers add scattered points of warm, cool contrast. The result is a high‑contrast, haunting image that feels both royal and tragic.

The chariot shows natural imperfections: a small crack in the obsidian frame near the left wheel, a torn edge on the velvet curtain, a tiny chip on one of the throne’s gold finials. The wraiths have hairline cracks on their smooth heads, frayed robe edges, and uneven mist tendrils. A few glowing blue flowers are crushed under the chariot’s shadow. These details reinforce the hyper‑realistic mandate.

TITLE OF THE SERIES
ROUGH WAVES — The Ocean Goddess’s Tidal Chariot

CHAPTER / PIECE TITLE
Prologue — Ocean Oddity

IMAGE TYPE IDENTIFICATION
Single unified full-bleed 9:16 poster composition. No panels. No gutters. One continuous image with the visual and emotional force of a noble, mythic, oceanic tapestry rendered in hyper‑realistic, cinematic photography.

ART STYLE DECLARATION — SACRED MANTRA
4K, 8K, HD, UHD, hyperdetailed, raw photo, cinematic lighting, anamorphic lens, sharp focus, intricate surface texture, subsurface scattering on translucent materials, water, and glowing elements, visible micro‑scratches and natural imperfections, film grain, color graded, ARRI Alexa look, 85mm lens, f/1.8 aperture, masterpiece quality.

The visual identity is that of a high‑budget cinematic advertisement for a divine water‑gliding chariot‑ship pulled by mythical leviathans — every surface rendered with obsessive fidelity. The lighting is bright and ethereal, with warm golden highlights that define the chariot’s gold trim and cool, turquoise reflections from the water and leviathans. The color grading leans into a tropical, oceanic palette with mother‑of‑pearl white, seafoam green, aged gold, turquoise, and deep ocean blue. Film grain is present at 5% intensity, evenly distributed, adding texture without degrading the hyper‑detailed finish. The image must feel as though it was captured on a cinema camera with an anamorphic lens — slight oval bokeh in out‑of‑focus background elements, a subtle horizontal lens flare, and a shallow depth of field that keeps the chariot and leviathans razor‑sharp while the wave‑shaped rock formations behind them soften into atmospheric haze. Every material reads as real: polished mother‑of‑pearl, carved wood, brushed gold, shimmering scales, crystal‑clear water, and the faint scent of salt and sea spray.

The following elements are absolutely forbidden: 90s anime, cel animation, retro anime, VHS grain, scanlines, hand‑drawn line art, cel shading, pixelation, vector art, low resolution, flat colors, or any descriptor that suggests non‑photorealistic rendering.

CHARACTER REFERENCES — COMPLETE AND EXHAUSTIVE
Not applicable — no character appears. The chariot‑ship and two leviathans are the sole subjects. The following design references are locked for all elements of the image.

Primary Subject 1 — Chariot‑Ship (Scallop Throne on Wave Hull)

Overall Silhouette: A majestic, water‑gliding chariot‑ship with a sleek, curved hull shaped like a breaking wave. The hull is made of polished mother‑of‑pearl and seafoam green materials, with aged gold trim. At the center, a large, open scallop shell serves as the throne, facing forward. The throne has a high backrest with radiating shell ridges, upholstered in soft white silk. The ship has a low, graceful bow that curves upward like a cresting wave, and a stern with trailing silk ribbons in translucent white and pale turquoise. The ship glides on the water’s surface, leaving a gentle wake. Length approximately 8 meters, width 2.5 meters.

Color Palette: Mother‑of‑pearl white (hull, throne shell). Seafoam green (accent panels, interior lining). Aged gold (trim, railings, throne base). Translucent white and pale turquoise (silk ribbons, trailing fabric). Bright gold (small decorative sunbursts).

Hull & Wave Shape: The hull is narrow and elegant, with a pronounced upward curve at the bow, like the front of a wave crest. The surface is covered in overlapping mother‑of‑pearl scales (iridescent, with subtle pink, blue, and green reflections) and seafoam green lacquered wood panels. Gold trim outlines the deck edge. At the stern, a small, carved wave motif.

Throne (Scallop Shell): A large, open scallop shell (approximately 1.8 meters wide) is mounted on a raised dais at the center. The shell is mother‑of‑pearl with natural ridges radiating from the hinge. The interior is lined with soft, white silk padding. The throne faces forward, with no backrest above the shell’s natural height. Gold armrests curl outward like seahorse tails. Small turquoise gems are set into the shell’s edges.

Bow Ornament: At the prow, a golden sunburst or seashell ornament, with small dangling bells that catch the light.

Railings & Silk Ribbons: Low, golden railings line the deck, with balusters shaped like coral branches. From the stern, long, translucent silk ribbons (white and pale turquoise) trail into the water, floating and undulating as if moved by a gentle current. The ribbons are approximately 3 meters long.

Glow & Reflection: The mother‑of‑pearl hull reflects the sunlight and the surrounding sea. The water beneath the ship is calm, with a soft, golden reflection.

Primary Subject 2 — Two Colossal Leviathans (Sea Serpents)

Overall Silhouette: Two massive, serpentine sea creatures, each approximately 12 meters long from snout to tail tip, pulling the chariot‑ship via golden harnesses. They swim side‑by‑side, slightly staggered, with their bodies partially submerged but heads, necks, and upper backs visible above the water. Their bodies are covered in shimmering turquoise scales with iridescent blue and green highlights. They have long, undulating fin manes along their spines, flowing in the water. Their heads are dragon‑like, with elongated snouts, sharp teeth, and large, glowing cyan eyes. They have four powerful flippers (two visible each) and a long, paddle‑like tail fin.

Color Palette: Turquoise (primary scales). Iridescent blue and green (scale highlights). Glowing cyan (eyes, fin edges). Aged gold (harness, bridle). White (underbelly).

Head & Face: The head is reptilian, with a long, narrow snout, a row of sharp, white teeth visible in a slight gap. Two long, whisker‑like barbels extend from the chin, trailing back. The eyes are large, glowing a steady cyan (hex #00FFFF), with vertical slit pupils. Two small, curved horns of ivory sit above the eyes. The head scales are larger and more pronounced, with a subtle golden sheen.

Neck & Body: The neck is long and muscular, covered in overlapping scales. A fin mane runs from the top of the head down the spine, made of translucent membranes stretched over bony rays, glowing pale cyan at the edges. As the leviathans rise, portions of their back and flippers are visible. Their bodies taper to a long tail that ends in a large, vertical fin (like a whale or sea serpent).

Harness & Bridle: Each leviathan wears a golden bridle around its snout and head, with a central ring. A thick, braided rope of golden fiber (or gold‑plated chain) extends from the bridle back to the chariot’s bow, where it attaches to a heavy gold ring. The rope is taut, suggesting pulling force.

Water & Wake: The leviathans push through the water, creating a bow wave around their necks. Their backs are wet, with droplets of water sliding off their scales. Small splashes and ripples surround them.

Background Setting: A tropical bay with crystal‑clear turquoise water, under a bright blue sky with soft, wispy clouds. In the distance, towering wave‑shaped rock formations (stone waves) rise from the sea, covered in lush green vegetation. The far shore has a white sand beach and distant palm trees. The water is calm but shimmers with sunlight.

The water is crystal‑clear turquoise, with gentle ripples and reflections of the sun. In the distance, towering, wave‑shaped rock formations (stone waves) rise from the sea, their surfaces covered in lush green tropical vegetation and small waterfalls cascading into the bay. A white sand beach lines the far shore, with a few palm trees leaning over the water. Small white‑sailed ships dot the horizon. A thin, golden sunlight haze hangs in the air. The overall mood is one of serene, divine power — the domain of an ocean goddess.

Lower Two‑Thirds — The Central Figures (Chariot‑Ship + Two Leviathans)

The ensemble is captured in mid‑glide, moving from left to right across the frame. The two leviathans are in the foreground, side‑by‑side, their heads and necks rising above the water. Their glowing cyan eyes shine brightly. The golden ropes from their bridles stretch back to the chariot’s bow. The water around them splashes gently, with droplets catching the sunlight.

The chariot‑ship glides behind them, approximately four meters back. The scallop throne is empty, facing forward. The mother‑of‑pearl hull reflects the sky and water. The silk ribbons trail from the stern, floating on the surface. The golden railings catch the sun. The ship leaves a gentle, V‑shaped wake.

The lighting is bright and divine: the sun from above‑right provides warm, golden key light, creating sharp highlights on the mother‑of‑pearl and the leviathans’ wet scales. The water below reflects cool turquoise and white. The leviathans’ glowing eyes add a cool cyan accent. The result is a high‑contrast, luminous image that feels both powerful and serene.

The chariot shows natural imperfections: a few tiny scratches on the gold railing, a small water stain on the white silk cushion, a single seaweed strand caught on the bow ornament. The leviathans have a few scarred scales (from ancient battles), a barnacle on a fin edge, and water droplets on their snouts. These details reinforce the hyper‑realistic mandate.

Arranged as an integrated calligraphic composition over the lowest portion of the image, placed so it does not obscure the chariot’s bow or the water reflection. Soft white with a faint turquoise drop shadow.

TITLE OF THE SERIES
ABYSSAL DESCENT — The Abyssal Witch’s Living Maw

CHAPTER / PIECE TITLE
Prologue — Shadow Trap

IMAGE TYPE IDENTIFICATION
Single unified full-bleed 9:16 poster composition. No panels. No gutters. One continuous image with the visual and emotional force of a sinister, abyssal, living shadow tapestry rendered in hyper‑realistic, cinematic photography.

ART STYLE DECLARATION — SACRED MANTRA
4K, 8K, HD, UHD, hyperdetailed, raw photo, cinematic lighting, anamorphic lens, sharp focus, intricate surface texture, subsurface scattering on translucent energy, shadow mist, and glowing elements, visible micro‑scratches and natural imperfections, film grain, color graded, ARRI Alexa look, 85mm lens, f/1.8 aperture, masterpiece quality.

The visual identity is that of a high‑budget cinematic advertisement for a colossal, sentient shadow blob — every surface rendered with obsessive fidelity. The lighting is dramatic and abyssal, with intense violet and magenta highlights that define the entity’s jagged features and deep, absolute black shadows that emphasize its void‑like presence. The color grading leans into a dark, predatory palette with obsidian black, deep violet, glowing magenta, and touches of pale purple. Film grain is present at 5% intensity, evenly distributed, adding texture without degrading the hyper‑detailed finish. The image must feel as though it was captured on a cinema camera with an anamorphic lens — slight oval bokeh in out‑of‑focus background elements, a subtle horizontal lens flare, and a shallow depth of field that keeps the entity razor‑sharp while the abyssal rift and floating rocks behind it soften into atmospheric chaos. Every material reads as real: semi‑solid shadow, crackling energy, translucent smoke, and the faint hum of reality tearing.

The following elements are absolutely forbidden: 90s anime, cel animation, retro anime, VHS grain, scanlines, hand‑drawn line art, cel shading, pixelation, vector art, low resolution, flat colors, or any descriptor that suggests non‑photorealistic rendering.

CHARACTER REFERENCES — COMPLETE AND EXHAUSTIVE
Not applicable — no character appears. The sentient shadow blob entity is the sole subject. The following design references are locked for all elements of the image.

Primary Entity — LIVING MAW (Abyssal Shadow Blob)

Overall Silhouette: A colossal, sentient shadow blob – a swirling, living maw of obsidian smoke, jagged violet lightning, and glowing purple eyes. The entity is roughly 5 meters long and 3 meters wide, shaped like a demonic shark‑skull crossed with a dark nebula. It has a wide, tooth‑filled grin that stretches across its front, with rows of sharp, translucent teeth. Its body is amorphous, constantly shifting between solid shadow and gaseous mist. It floats silently approximately 1.5 meters above the ground, with no visible means of propulsion. The entity moves at blinding speed, leaving a rift of crackling abyssal energy in its wake. Its surface ripples like liquid shadow, with internal violet energy arcs visible through semi‑transparent areas. Its eyes are large, oval, and glow with a predatory magenta light.

Color Palette: Obsidian black (primary body). Deep violet (internal energy, shadow mist highlights). Glowing magenta (eyes, teeth edges, energy arcs). Pale purple (wisps, aura). Dark grey (highlights on tooth ridges).

Body & Texture: The entity is not solid; it is a semi‑solid mass of condensed shadow and dark magic. Its surface has a liquid, oily sheen, with occasional ripples and bubbles of darker black. Patches of translucent shadow reveal a swirling, chaotic interior of violet energy and tiny stars (like a mini‑nebula). The body is roughly ovoid but asymmetrical, with trailing tendrils of shadow mist that whip behind it. The front is flattened into a shark‑like snout, with the enormous mouth cutting across the width.

Mouth & Teeth: The mouth is a wide, crescent‑shaped gash filled with rows of sharp, translucent teeth. The teeth are pale purple to near‑white, with jagged edges. Some teeth are longer, forming fangs. The interior of the mouth glows with a deep magenta light, as if it contains a core of pure abyssal energy. Small wisps of shadow smoke escape from between the teeth.

Eyes: The entity has two large, oval eyes set high on the front of the “head.” They glow with a steady, intense magenta light (hex #FF00AA), with no visible pupils. The eyes are recessed in shadowy sockets, but their glow illuminates the surrounding surface. Small, flickering violet arcs occasionally jump from the eye corners.

Trailing Tendrils & Mist: From the rear of the body, dozens of long, semi‑transparent tendrils of shadow mist trail backward, each up to 3 meters long. The tendrils undulate as if in a wind, dissipating into nothing at their tips. Small violet sparks and dark embers drift off the tendrils. The entity is also surrounded by a constant, faint aura of dark mist that clings to its underside.

Internal Energy Arcs: Across the entity’s body, especially near the front and around the eyes, visible arcs of violet and magenta lightning crackle and jump between different points on the surface. These arcs are irregular, flickering, and leave faint afterimages.

Riding Surface: The back of the entity is relatively flat and stable, with a slight depression (like a saddle) made of denser shadow. The surface is smooth but has a subtle, organic texture — like polished obsidian. No rider is present.

Lighting & Glow Effects: The magenta eyes and the mouth interior provide the primary light sources. The internal violet arcs add pulsing highlights. The trailing mist has a soft, pale purple glow.

Aura & Atmosphere: The entity is surrounded by a faint, crackling energy field that distorts the air around it. Small shadow particles (like dark snow) fall from its body and dissolve before hitting the ground. The ground beneath it is lit by a dim, magenta underglow.

Background Setting: A shattered, rune‑etched stone platform floating in an abyssal rift. The sky is a swirling vortex of deep violet, magenta, and black, with a massive “Abyssal Eye” (a dark energy vortex) in the distance, crackling with violet lightning. Jagged floating rock islands and broken pillars drift in the void. The ground is a calcified, bone‑like surface etched with glowing violet veins and strange, crystalline flora that glows with an eerie purple luminescence.

The crystalline flora on the ground adds scattered points of purple light. The result is a high‑contrast, terrifying image that feels like a living nightmare.

The entity shows natural imperfections: a few small, dark ripples on its surface that look like scars, a slightly uneven tooth row (one missing tooth), a faint tear in a trailing tendril, and a small, static spark that clings to its side. These details reinforce the hyper‑realistic mandate.

“THE ABYSSAL WITCH’S LIVING MAW” rendered slightly smaller, same calligraphic style but more restrained, soft pale purple with obsidian black shadows.

TITLE OF THE SERIES

IMAGE TYPE IDENTIFICATION

ART STYLE DECLARATION — SACRED MANTRA
4K, 8K, HD, UHD, hyperdetailed, raw photo, cinematic lighting, anamorphic lens, sharp focus, intricate surface texture, subsurface scattering on translucent materials, glowing energy, and paint reflections, visible micro‑scratches and natural imperfections, film grain, color graded, ARRI Alexa look, 85mm lens, f/1.8 aperture, masterpiece quality.

The visual identity is that of a high‑budget cinematic advertisement for a futuristic, bulkier sports car equipped with grappling systems and twin blade motifs — every surface rendered with obsessive fidelity. The lighting is dramatic and golden‑hour, with warm golden highlights that define the car’s sharp, armored edges and cool, violet reflections that emphasize its sleek, predatory nature. The color grading leans into a regal, high‑tech palette with deep purple, silver, gold, glowing blue‑violet, and touches of dark charcoal. Film grain is present at 5% intensity, evenly distributed, adding texture without degrading the hyper‑detailed finish. The following elements are absolutely forbidden: 90s anime, cel animation, retro anime, VHS grain, scanlines, hand‑drawn line art, cel shading, pixelation, vector art, low resolution, flat colors, or any descriptor that suggests non‑photorealistic rendering.

CHARACTER REFERENCES — COMPLETE AND EXHAUSTIVE
Not applicable — no character appears. The sports car is the sole subject. The following design references are locked for all elements of the image.

Primary Car — CUT THROAT (Blade Dancer Aerial Coupe)

Overall Silhouette: A super futuristic, sleek sports car with a low, wide, bulkier stance — like a cyber‑samurai battle coupe. Two retractable grappling cable launchers are mounted on the front fenders, with glowing blue cable tips visible. The wheels are large, with deep purple rim LEDs and high‑performance tires. The car is approximately 4.8 meters long, 2.1 meters wide, and sits low to the ground.

Color Palette: Deep purple (primary body). Silver (armor panels, blade wings, cable housings). Gold (trim, front crest, wheel accents). Glowing blue‑violet (headlights, cable tips, rim LEDs, underglow). Matte charcoal (underside, rear diffuser). High‑gloss black (canopy).

Front View: The front is low and wide, with a sharp, pointed nose. Two large, angled LED headlights (hex #6A5ACD – slate blue‑violet) sit in recessed housings, giving a fierce, focused expression. Side Profile (Left Side): The profile is long and muscular, with pronounced wheel arches. The lower door has a silver armor plate with gold rivets. The roof is a fighter‑jet style canopy (dark tinted glass, gold frame), with a sharp dorsal fin running from the roof to the rear. A thin, braided steel cable is visible, coiled inside. The rear deck has a second blade wing (smaller) integrated into the trunk line. The wheels have five thick, twisted spokes in silver with glowing purple rim LEDs.

Rear View: The rear is wide, with a full‑width LED taillight bar (blue‑violet). The twin blade wings are visible: one large upper wing, one lower diffuser‑integrated wing. The rear diffuser is massive, with vertical silver strakes. The exhaust is a central, trapezoidal outlet with a gold surround, venting a faint blue flame. Grappling Cable Launcher (used in afterimage): A macro view of the launcher: a silver cylinder (15 cm diameter) with a blue‑glowing circular aperture. Blade Rear Wings (used in afterimage): A detailed view of the upper wing: shaped like a curved katana blade, silver with a sharp gold edge. The wing is mounted on a slim, carbon fiber stalk that can rotate. A faint blue energy aura surrounds the wingtip.

Fighter‑Jet Canopy & Dorsal Fin (used in afterimage): A close‑up of the roof: the dark tinted glass canopy with a gold frame, no visible interior detail. The dorsal fin is a sharp, silver blade‑like ridge running from the back of the canopy to the rear window.

Wheel & Rim (used in afterimage): A macro view of the front wheel: five twisted silver spokes, a deep purple rim with glowing blue‑violet LED strip, and a gold center cap with a blade emblem. The tire is low‑profile with a subtle purple sidewall stripe.

Lighting & Effects: The blue‑violet headlights cast a focused beam. The cable launcher aperture glows faintly blue. The rim LEDs glow. The exhaust vents a subtle blue flame. A faint, cool blue underglow illuminates the marble platform.

Aura & Atmosphere: The car is surrounded by a faint, shimmering energy field (kinetic aura). Small, digital particles (like data streams) drift from the cable launchers. The car’s reflection is visible in the polished marble.

Background Setting: A floating marble platform of a celestial fortress at dawn. Massive, sword‑shaped pillars of white stone and gold filigree rise around. Long, blue banners flutter in a gentle breeze. A soft golden glow from the rising sun catches the mist. In the distance, floating buttresses and arched bridges disappear into a sea of clouds.

The purple rim LEDs glow softly. The car sits low on the polished marble, with a faint blue underglow.

The lighting is warm and dramatic: the rising sun from behind‑right provides golden backlight, creating a soft halo around the car’s dorsal fin and rear wing. The car’s own blue‑violet headlights and underglow provide cool, focused key light from the front. The marble reflects both warm and cool tones. The result is a high‑contrast, heroic image that feels both futuristic and noble.

The car shows natural imperfections: a few tiny scratches on the silver door armor, a small smudge on the headlight lens, a thin layer of dust on the rear diffuser, and a single blue banner thread caught on the cable launcher. These details reinforce the hyper‑realistic mandate.

Lower Quarter — Typography and Calligraphic Elements

Arranged as an integrated calligraphic composition over the lowest portion of the image, placed so it does not obscure the car’s front wheel or the marble reflection. The typography is rendered as sharp, high‑resolution text with subtle metallic textures and drop shadows, not hand‑drawn, but styled to evoke traditional Japanese seal and calligraphy forms (digitally perfected).

Hero’s Name (Car Model):
“CUT THROAT” lettered in large, semi‑calligraphic English text — thick‑and‑thin brush‑inspired strokes, rendered in blue‑violet with a faint gold outline. The letters are spaced wide, sharp, lethal.

Formal Title (below name):
“THE BLADE DANCER’S AERIAL COUPE” rendered slightly smaller, same calligraphic style but more restrained, soft silver with deep purple shadows.

Hanko Seal (below title):
An inked vermilion square seal, slightly imperfect edges as if hand‑carved, but rendered in photorealistic 4K. The seal contains a stylized twin blades crossed with a grappling hook — Fanny’s personal emblem adapted for the car.

Catchphrase (below seal):
“The sky is my road. The blade is my signature.” Lettered in a smaller, elegant serif font with a slight italic tilt, faded silver with a blue glow.

Dialogue Quote (below catchphrase):
“They threw me from the clouds. I learned to fly. Now I hunt them from the ground.” Rendered in a slightly different typography — a clean, futuristic sans‑serif with a subtle metallic texture — to distinguish it as spoken or thematic text. Soft white with a faint violet drop shadow.

Entire Image Mood: Fast, noble, lethal, and undeniably cinematic. A main poster for a blade dancer’s futuristic sports car — the kind that would be displayed in a sky fortress hangar.

TECHNICAL SPECIFICATIONS FOR IMAGE GENERATION
Aspect Ratio: 9:16 (vertical)

Art Style: Photorealistic cinematic rendering (as defined in Sacred Mantra)

Format: Single unified full-bleed composition — no panels, no gutters

Resolution: 8K (7680x13653 pixels equivalent), also compatible with 4K and HD downscales

Camera & Lens: 85mm prime lens at f/1.8, focused on the central car and its cable launcher. Shallow depth of field — background fortress and afterimages slightly soft but legible.

Visible metallic paint with flake, brushed silver on launcher and wings, polished gold on crest, carbon fiber on diffuser, rubber on tires, and marble stone. Floating digital particles and dust motes.

TITLE OF THE SERIES
PHANTOM EXECUTION — The Perfumed Knight’s Rose Chariot

CHAPTER / PIECE TITLE
Prologue — Thorned Rose

IMAGE TYPE IDENTIFICATION
Single unified full-bleed 9:16 poster composition. No panels. No gutters. One continuous image with the visual and emotional force of a divine, noble, rose‑infused tapestry rendered in hyper‑realistic, cinematic photography.

ART STYLE DECLARATION — SACRED MANTRA
4K, 8K, HD, UHD, hyperdetailed, raw photo, cinematic lighting, anamorphic lens, sharp focus, intricate surface texture, subsurface scattering on translucent materials, rose petals, and glowing elements, visible micro‑scratches and natural imperfections, film grain, color graded, ARRI Alexa look, 85mm lens, f/1.8 aperture, masterpiece quality.

The visual identity is that of a high‑budget cinematic advertisement for a divine chariot pulled by winged unicorns — every surface rendered with obsessive fidelity. The lighting is warm and ethereal, with soft golden and rose highlights that define the chariot’s elegant curves and deep, noble shadows that emphasize its regal, romantic presence. The color grading leans into a celestial, aristocratic palette with white gold, rose gold, sapphire blue, deep crimson, and soft pink. Film grain is present at 5% intensity, evenly distributed, adding texture without degrading the hyper‑detailed finish. The image must feel as though it was captured on a cinema camera with an anamorphic lens — slight oval bokeh in out‑of‑focus background elements, a subtle horizontal lens flare, and a shallow depth of field that keeps the chariot and unicorns razor‑sharp while the celestial courtyard and sword‑shaped pillars behind them soften into atmospheric grandeur. Every material reads as real: polished white gold, rose gold, velvet, crystal, feathers, and the faint scent of roses and incense.

The following elements are absolutely forbidden: 90s anime, cel animation, retro anime, VHS grain, scanlines, hand‑drawn line art, cel shading, pixelation, vector art, low resolution, flat colors, or any descriptor that suggests non‑photorealistic rendering.

CHARACTER REFERENCES — COMPLETE AND EXHAUSTIVE
Not applicable — no character appears. The chariot and two winged unicorns are the sole subjects. The following design references are locked for all elements of the image.

Primary Subject 1 — Divine Rose Chariot (Noble Bloom)

Overall Silhouette: A majestic, open chariot shaped like an unfolding rose bloom, mounted on two large wheels. The chariot body is a curved, petal‑like platform of white gold and rose gold, with a high‑backed throne at the center. The throne is adorned with sapphire blue velvet cushions and gold rose motifs. The chariot has a delicate canopy of intertwining rose vines (gold and crystal) over the throne, from which small, glowing pink crystals hang like dewdrops. The wheels are large, with petal‑shaped spokes and a soft pink aura. The chariot floats approximately 50 cm above a polished marble courtyard. Length approximately 4 meters, width 2.2 meters.

Color Palette: White gold (primary frame). Rose gold (trim, rose motifs, wheel spokes). Sapphire blue (throne cushions, velvet drapes). Deep crimson (rose petals, vine accents). Soft pink (wheel aura, crystal glow). Gold (harness chains).

Chariot Body & Throne: The platform is a layered, petal‑like shape — overlapping curved panels of white gold with rose gold edging. The throne is high‑backed, with a backrest shaped like a half‑bloomed rose, upholstered in sapphire blue velvet with gold rose‑shaped buttons. The armrests end in golden rosebuds. A light, open canopy of intertwined rose vines arches over the throne, made of rose gold wire and crystal leaves. Small, pink, teardrop crystals hang from the vines, swaying gently.

Wheels & Petal Spokes: Two large wheels (1.5 meters diameter) at the rear. Each wheel has six petal‑shaped spokes (curved, overlapping like rose petals) made of rose gold. The rim is white gold. A soft pink aura glows between the spokes, creating a luminous halo. The wheels hover, not touching the ground.

Harness & Chains: From the front yoke, two thin, gleaming gold chains extend to the unicorns’ bridles. The chains have small rose‑shaped links.

Rose Vines & Petals: Trailing from the chariot’s rear and sides are long, thin rose vines of rose gold wire, with small, deep crimson enamel leaves and tiny glowing pink crystal rosebuds. Actual rose petals (deep crimson and soft pink) drift in the air around the chariot, as if carried by a gentle magical breeze.

Glow & Reflection: The pink aura from the wheels casts a soft glow on the marble. The crystals under the canopy twinkle.

Primary Subject 2 — Two Winged Unicorns (Divine Pullers)

Overall Silhouette: Two pure white unicorns, side‑by‑side, with massive feathered angelic wings, golden hooves, and flowing rose‑pink manes and tails. They stand approximately 1.8 meters at the shoulder, with a proud, arched neck posture. Their wings are spread slightly, feathers catching the light. Their eyes are a warm, sapphire blue, gentle yet noble. They are harnessed with gold bridles and breastplates.

Color Palette: Pure white (coat, feathers). Rose pink (mane, tail, wing edge highlights). Gold (hooves, bridle, horn). Sapphire blue (eyes). Soft silver (wing feather edges).

Head & Horn: The head is elegant, with a straight profile, soft muzzle, and large, expressive sapphire blue eyes. A single, spiraling horn of polished gold (approximately 30 cm) grows from the forehead, glowing faintly with a warm golden light. The mane is long, flowing, and rose‑pink, with soft waves.

Wings: Two large wings per unicorn (each wing span approximately 3 meters). Feathers are layered, pure white with a faint rose‑pink iridescence on the edges. The wings are semi‑spread, as if ready to lift off. Small, glowing pink sparkles fall from the wingtips.

Legs & Hooves: Legs are slender but muscular, ending in golden hooves that hover just above the ground. The hooves have a soft, warm glow.

Harness & Bridle: A golden bridle with a central rose‑shaped medallion, and a matching breastplate of gold and rose gold. The gold chains from the chariot attach to rings on the breastplate.

Aura & Effects: Each unicorn is surrounded by a faint, warm golden aura. Tiny rose petals and pink sparkles drift from their manes and wings.

Background Setting: A celestial marble courtyard of a floating fortress at golden hour. Massive, sword‑shaped pillars of white stone and gold filigree rise on both sides, draped with sapphire blue banners. The floor is polished white marble with rose gold inlays. A soft, golden sunrise glow fills the sky, with a few wispy pink clouds. In the distance, floating buttresses and arched bridges disappear into a sea of golden mist. Red and pink rose petals drift through the air.

The Canvas (9:16 vertical):
Upper Quarter — The Afterimage Array (Chariot & Unicorn Components)

Filling the upper portion of the background, from approximately the top edge down to the main chariot’s canopy level, are four ghosted afterimages of the Phantom Execution’s critical components. These afterimages are rendered semi‑transparent (approximately 40% opacity), floating in the golden air like noble blueprints. Each afterimage is labeled with small, elegant calligraphic text integrated into the composition (sharp, high‑resolution, not hand‑drawn). Each afterimage shows a different part of the ensemble in exquisite detail.

All afterimages are positioned so they do not obstruct the central chariot and unicorns but instead frame them, creating a sense of divine, romantic engineering.

Middle‑Upper Background — Celestial Courtyard & Sword Pillars (Photorealistic Rendering)

Behind and between the afterimages, the background is a hyper‑realistic depiction of a celestial courtyard at golden hour. Polished white marble floors with rose gold inlays extend toward a distant, glowing horizon. Massive, sword‑shaped pillars of white stone and gold filigree rise on both sides, draped with long, sapphire blue banners that flutter gently. In the distance, floating buttresses and arched bridges connect to other platforms, all disappearing into a sea of soft, golden mist. The sky is a warm gradient from pale gold near the horizon to soft lavender above, with wispy pink clouds. Red and pink rose petals drift through the air, catching the sunlight. The overall mood is one of serene, noble beauty — a garden of the gods.

Lower Two‑Thirds — The Central Figures (Chariot + Two Winged Unicorns)

The ensemble is captured in mid‑glide, moving from left to right across the frame. The two winged unicorns are in the foreground, side‑by‑side, their heads held high, their wings partially spread. Their rose‑pink manes flow in an unfelt wind. Their golden horns glow softly. Small rose petals and pink sparkles drift from their wings and manes.

The chariot floats behind them, approximately three meters back. The rose‑shaped throne is empty, facing forward. The petal wheels hover, their pink aura casting a soft glow on the marble. The crystal canopy twinkles. Trailing rose vines with crimson leaves and pink crystal buds sway gently. The gold chains are taut.

The lighting is warm and divine: the golden sunrise from behind‑right provides soft, warm backlighting, creating a halo effect around the unicorns’ wings and the chariot’s canopy. The chariot’s pink wheel aura and the unicorns’ glowing horns provide cool, pink‑gold key light from the front. The drifting rose petals add scattered points of color. The result is a high‑contrast, romantic image that feels both noble and ethereal.

The chariot shows natural imperfections: a tiny scratch on the white gold platform near the left wheel, a small petal missing from the throne’s rose motif, a faint water spot on a crystal droplet. The unicorns have a few loose feathers, a slight scuff on a golden hoof, and a single rose petal caught in a mane. These details reinforce the hyper‑realistic mandate.

Lower Quarter — Typography and Calligraphic Elements

Arranged as an integrated calligraphic composition over the lowest portion of the image, placed so it does not obscure the chariot’s front wheels or the marble reflection. The typography is rendered as sharp, high‑resolution text with subtle metallic textures and drop shadows, not hand‑drawn, but styled to evoke traditional Japanese seal and calligraphy forms (digitally perfected).

Hero’s Name (Chariot Name):
“PHANTOM EXECUTION” lettered in large, semi‑calligraphic English text — thick‑and‑thin brush‑inspired strokes, rendered in rose gold with a faint sapphire outline. The letters are spaced wide, elegant, lethal.

Formal Title (below name):
“THE PERFUMED KNIGHT’S ROSE CHARIOT” rendered slightly smaller, same calligraphic style but more restrained, soft white gold with deep crimson shadows.

An inked vermilion square seal, slightly imperfect edges as if hand‑carved, but rendered in photorealistic 4K. The seal contains a stylized rose crossed with a rapier — Lancelot’s personal emblem adapted for the chariot.

Catchphrase (below seal):
